> **Status:** revision 3 (rev3), 2026-09-26. Rev3 applies your round-2 decisions of 2026-09-26: **OD-C8** decided (the ~7.6 GB MERFISH-C57BL6J-638850 download to `/srv/storage` is approved for M6b, and the pre-registered selection rule picks among variants (a)–(d), (f)); **OD-E1 / OD-E9** decided — *simulation only* (new panels are validated only by in-silico simulation from the Allen references; no public validation dataset is downloaded; real in-house datasets get label-free production QC that can only downgrade trust or warn); **OD-E2** decided = (b); **OD-E4** decided (finer levels report-only and opt-in; leaves stay WHB supercluster / WMB subclass). The other rev2 open items (OD-B13, OD-D11, OD-D12, OD-E3, OD-E5–OD-E8) stay open with the recommended default adopted unless you object. Everything else is unchanged from rev2 except the held-out-donor correction (§3.2): your first-round decisions (evidence archived; OD-A3, OD-B1, OD-B2, OD-D5, OD-D6; mouse coronal AP-axis scope; panel/platform generality in v1), the adopted recommendations and the two rev2 checks (Appendix A). After the rev3 check, gate P was made passable at the sample sizes the references supply and is recorded per (level, class), so promotion never changes what is emitted (§14, §8.2, §4.7); M13 is re-estimated at 7–10 developer-days (Appendix A, "Rev3 check"). The M0-docs `[docs]` PR commits this plan to `main` as `docs/plans/robust-celltype-annotation-plan.md`, together with the branching notes, before the integration branch is cut (§2.2).

# MerXen implementation plan (rev3): robust, reference-based cell-type annotation and a quality-robust hierarchy

- **Effort name:** `robust-celltype-annotation` ("rca"). **Date and base:** 2026-09-26; repo HEAD `636ac87` on `main`.
- **Branching (OD-D5, decided):** one long-lived integration branch, `feature/robust-celltype-annotation`, holds all work; each milestone is a PR into it from `feature/rca-m<N>-<slug>`. Exception: the M0 data-integrity fixes go straight to `main` (`bugfix/rca-m0<x>-<slug>`) and are then merged into the integration branch. What merges to `main`, and when: §2.2.
- **Overlapping work (OD-D6, decided):** this work goes first. `feature/gaston` (+4,236 lines in 25 files; merge-base `f8f6204`, 2026-08-06, 58 commits behind `main`) rebases onto it later; hook points, conflict hotspots and a rebase guide are in §2.4.
- **Files:** this plan `$A/plan_rev3/final_plan_rev3.txt` (`docs/plans/robust-celltype-annotation-plan.md` (committed in M0-docs); before the rev3-check fixes `final_plan_rev3.pre_fix.txt`); rev2 `$A/plan_rev2/robust-celltype-annotation-plan.rev2.md` (= `final_plan_rev2.md`; `final_plan_rev2.txt` is the pre-user-edit copy, without the (e) withdrawal; pre-check version `revised_plan.txt`); rev1 `$A/plan/final_plan.txt`; draft `$A/plan/draft_plan.txt`; progress logs `$A/plan/PROGRESS.txt`, `$A/plan_rev2/PROGRESS_{reviser,checker,reviewer_generality,final_reviser}.txt`, `$A/plan_rev3/PROGRESS_{reviser,verifier,fixer}_rev3.txt`; rev2 analyses `$A/plan_rev2/ap_axis/`, `$A/plan_rev2/review_generality/`; rev3 edit scripts `$A/plan_rev3/rev3_*.py` (`rev3_edits.py` builds the pre-fix file from rev2, `rev3_fixes.py` applies the rev3-check fixes).
- **Paths:** `$A` = `/srv/storage/MerXen/annotation_dev/evidence_20260926`, the evidence archive (6,065 files, 38 GB, `$A/MANIFEST.sha256` verified). Every evidence path below is relative to `$A`. *History:* the evidence was produced under the session scratch `$S` (`/tmp/claude-1001/.../scratchpad/ctplan`, now deleted); archived headers still name `$S` paths — read `$S/<rel>` as `$A/<rel>`.
- **Abbreviations:** MMC = MapMyCells (`cell_type_mapper`, ctm); bp = MMC bootstrap probability; LL = in-house count-likelihood typer; M / X = MERSCOPE / Xenium side of a pair; HO = held-out donor; JSD = Jensen–Shannon *distance*, base 2 (`exp/E1/e1lib.py:138`); "table cells" = objects with ≥ 10 counts after control removal (the clustered H5AD / zarr table); AP = CCF anterior–posterior coordinate in mm (ABC `x_ccf`, 0 = anterior); OB / CB = olfactory bulb / cerebellum; panel = the **declared** gene list (vendor panel file or source table), resolved to IDs and identified by `panel_hash`; panel family = that panel plus, for trust only, panels of the same species and platform with Jaccard ≥ 0.95 that contain all its root markers (§8.1).
- **Confidence tags:** [H] measured or verified in code; [M] measured, but the proxy is imperfect or circular; [L] design judgement or extrapolation, validated in acceptance.

**Changes in rev3** (your round-2 decisions, 2026-09-26; Appendix A, "Rev3 changes").
- **OD-C8 decided:** the MERFISH-638850 expression download (~7.6 GB, public Allen data, to `/srv/storage`) is approved for M6b; the pre-registered rule (direct harm, leave-neighbours-out region shares) picks among variants (a)–(d), (f); (e) stays withdrawn (§7.8, M6b, §10).
- **OD-E1 / OD-E9 decided — simulation only** (not the rev2 recommendation): no public dataset is downloaded to validate new panels (§8.8 rewritten). Gate P now promotes a family `provisional` → `validated` on pre-registered **simulation** criteria NP1–NP9 (§14) — precision and coverage at the panel's depth, stability across held-out donors and seeds with frozen thresholds, resolvability consistency, sensitivity to contamination and gene-efficiency perturbations, error structure — with stricter margins than the real-data-validated panels, which simulation-validated families keep in production (`validation_basis`, §8.2, §4.7). Real in-house datasets still get the label-free production QC (dataset gate, G1, marker referee, paired-platform concordance, flag rates), which can only downgrade or warn (`real_qc.py`). M13 rescoped (7–10 developer-days for the simulation code, the per-class gate-P evaluation and its dry run on the seeded panels, the QC module, tests and first-family runs, plus ~2.5–3.5 h CPU per family of up to 815 genes; the vendor-cell input, downloads and re-segmentation are dropped, §17); risks R42–R45 added and R41 withdrawn; OD-D12's segmentation control filter is fixed on `main` from the 10x / Vizgen documentation before the first 5K or custom dataset is segmented, and that dataset's feature types only confirm it (§8.4).
- **OD-E2 decided = (b)** and **OD-E4 decided** (report-only, opt-in fine levels): both were already the plan's defaults, so only their status changes.
- **Correction found while grounding rev3** [H]: the default held-out donor H19.30.002 has the *fewest* frontal WHB cells (32,606 of 125,481; H18.30.002 51,983; H19.30.001 40,892), not the most as rev2 said; `"auto"` is redefined accordingly, so the pilot recipe (`exp/E2/ho/`) is unchanged (§3.2, §5.7).
- **After the rev3 check** (Appendix A, "Rev3 check"): gate P is scored on sets of ≥ 200 confident calls per (level, class, depth), pooled over the held-out donors or draws, with deep bins pooled and inherited; it requires point precision ≥ target⁺ with a Wilson bound ≥ target, and tests the spread across replicates against sampling error. Validation is recorded per (level, class) (`validated_panel_levels.csv`); promotion never changes what is emitted; the criteria are dry-run on set a, ag7 and VZG2 before any new family is scored (§14, §8.2, §4.7; R45). Real-data QC only warns on the seeded families until their species gate (§8.8, H8). Simulation-validated floors carry no warning (§5.4). M13's mouse QC waits for M6b. The Zhuang series needs its own download approval (§7.8).

**Changes in rev2.**
- Evidence archived (OD-A1 done; M-1 step 1 done; R2 closed). All `$S` paths now point to `$A`.
- Decided: mapped leaves (WHB supercluster / WMB subclass), de novo opt-in v1.1 (OD-B1); WHB frontal primary + SEA-AD Multiregion required second vote, concordance and disease-state reference (OD-B2); snapshot + `_mapfirst` key until each species flips (OD-A3); one integration branch with M0 on `main` (OD-D5, §2.2); this work first, GASTON rebases, new code in new files behind marked hook points (OD-D6, §2.4).
- **Mouse scope:** coronal sections along the whole AP axis except CB and OB. The validated panel precompute is WMB-10Xv3 only and covers all 13 regions, so no 10Xv2 download is needed (§7.1). New per-AP-bin validation (M6b) and criteria MO10–MO11 (§7.8).
- **Panel/platform generality is v1** (§8): gene-ID resolution for any panel; coverage diagnostics; a per-panel resolvability self-map in `ANNOTATE_REFERENCE_PREP` replacing `never_emit_levels`; trust states that fail safe; 5K compute guards; mouse on Xenium; new-panel acceptance NP1–NP9; milestones M3b and M13. The v1.1 simulator and its isotonic local-threshold step move into v1; calibrated-probability output, measured contamination and platform factors stay v1.1 (OD-E2).
- **After the rev2 checks** (Appendix A): timeline includes M6 and M6b; M0 parameters live on `main`; the plan reaches `main` in M0-docs; gate verdict = level + warning flag; per-cell (local) isotonic thresholds reweighted to each dataset's composition; resolvability only at depths the test cells support; declared-panel families; exact-case species test; direct AP-axis harm metric with leave-neighbours-out region shares; measured 5K memory; vendor-cell input for public datasets; the decision list now holds every decision point (final section).

**Changes from the pre-review draft (rev1; details in Appendix A):** staged rollout (human flip M8, mouse M9; LL, calibrated probabilities and de novo splits in v1.1); mapped leaves; soft composition over all table cells; report-only contamination and diffuse flags with a dataset-empirical null; the validated mouse configuration plus a mouse gate with the invalid VZG2 segmentation as negative control; no `storeDir`; byte-identical legacy Nextflow script text; ANNOTATE split into MAP and RESOLVE; `uns` holds JSON strings only; thin conda install with a lock-hash header; metro map updated in every milestone; MAPMYCELLS disabled in `rowSampleSettings`; pre-registered acceptance with held-out sets and non-circular checks.

## 0. Executive summary (for the PI)

1. **Done / next.** Evidence is archived to `$A` (6,065 files, 38 GB, sha256 verified; the `/tmp` copy is gone). Still to do (OD-A2 adopted, OD-A3 decided): snapshot the SSD1 MapMyCells cache and pause `region`/`both` runs until M0b; snapshot legacy outputs before the first map_first run.
2. **The legacy labeller is broken by construction:** legacy `broad_class` MERSCOPE–Xenium JSD .51 / 1.00 / .66 / .44 (P7513 / P1212 / P7113 / P5011); P1212 MERSCOPE has 0 neurons.
3. **Human: per-cell MapMyCells on Allen WHB frontal** (supercluster → cluster) with panel-aware markers; SEA-AD Multiregion is the required second vote, concordance and disease-state reference (OD-B2, decided). JSD .13 / .14 (P7513 / P1212, all cells); 0.3–0.6% implausible calls.
4. **Every cell gets a status per level and a deepest-confident label;** a dataset gate makes P1212 and P5011 MERSCOPE broad-only. Composition is estimated softly over all cells (JSD .14 / .13); confident-only doubles the JSD.
5. **Map-first hierarchy (OD-B1, decided):** branch = broad class (neurons by NT); leaf = confident WHB supercluster (human) / WMB subclass (mouse); "unresolved" explicit. No Leiden resolution decides structure; de novo within-type splits are opt-in v1.1. RESOLUTE is not adopted.
6. **New: panel/platform generality is v1** (Xenium 5K, custom Xenium v1, custom MERSCOPE; human and mouse). Each panel gets gene-ID resolution, coverage diagnostics and a **resolvability self-map** in reference prep (held-out reference cells restricted to the panel, thinned, contaminated, mapped with the production configuration). It decides which levels the panel may emit, for which classes and at which depths, and raises thresholds (E2's per-cell isotonic rule) and floors where needed, replacing hard-coded `never_emit_levels`; each dataset's decisions are reweighted to its own composition.
7. **Unknown panels fail safe:** trust states refused / broad-only / provisional / validated; provisional panels get stricter targets and a banner. **New panels are validated by simulation only** (OD-E1 / OD-E9, decided 2026-09-26): a family becomes `validated`, level by level and class by class, when pre-registered simulation criteria NP1–NP9 pass — precision and coverage at the panel's depth, stability across held-out donors and seeds, resolvability consistency, robustness to contamination and gene-efficiency perturbations — with stricter margins than the real-data-validated panels, kept in production because simulation is optimistic (E2: up to 5–10 points below ~60 counts on Xenium). No public validation data are downloaded; real in-house datasets get label-free QC that can only downgrade trust or warn. The simulator and its isotonic threshold step are in v1 (OD-E2 = (b), decided); calibrated probabilities, measured contamination and platform factors stay v1.1.
8. **Mouse:** WMB panel-aware markers with the Allen means all validation used; automatic region inference plus two-tier pruning; a label-free gate that must fail the invalid VZG2 segmentation. Scope: coronal sections along the whole AP axis except CB and OB; WMB-10Xv3 covers every region, so no 10Xv2 download.
9. **AP-axis gap (new analysis of E7):** on the 38 in-scope MERFISH sections, the auto-region rule wrongly drops ≤ 0.055% of a section's cells behind AP 6 mm (mean ≤ 0.01%) but up to 0.72% in front (at AP 3.3 a missed OB sliver drops the whole OB-IMN class, mostly RMS cells in the AON); counted directly as confident Allen cells in dropped nodes, the anterior maximum is 0.70% and every other bin ≤ 0.11%. M6b validates the production rule per AP bin, out of sample, on the MERFISH-638850 data whose download you approved (OD-C8), and hardens it before the mouse flip (MO10–MO11).
10. **Contamination is pervasive** (2.6–11% of counts on reference-absent genes); it is a score, never used to drop cells.
11. **M0 fixes go straight to `main`:** VZG2 segmentation offset; cache `rmtree`; ctm 1.5.5 in Nextflow envs; GPU/CPU Leiden provenance; 4 genes without Ensembl IDs. Everything else lives on one integration branch (a PR per milestone) that merges to `main` at acceptance gates, human first; GASTON rebases afterwards onto a few marked hook points.
12. **Timeline [L]:** standalone labels for existing results at M3 (about 12–16 developer-days); human flip at M8 (about 35–48); mouse flip at M9 (+9.5–13, i.e. M6 + M6b + M9); new-panel onboarding with simulation-only validation M13 (7–10, in parallel); v1.1 later.
13. **Compute (CPU only):** a human pair (4 segmentations) about 1.0–1.7 h, a mouse section about 55 min, reference prep 1–2 h per validated panel. A 5K mouse panel: hours of prep, ~60 GB of reference markers on disk, and a query-marker step whose memory grows ~22 MB per gene (~120–170 GB at 5K, ~53–66 GB with the prefilter) [L]; M3b measures it before any 5K run (OD-E8); bundles cached per panel family. Gate-P simulation (M13) is CPU only: ~2.5–3.5 h per ≤ 815-gene family, ~6–18 h per 5K family, plus a one-off ~8–10 h dry run on the seeded panels [L]; no public data are downloaded or re-segmented.
14. **Legacy stays the default** until pre-registered criteria pass, including held-out donors (P7113, P5011) and a second segmentation. Decided in round 2 (2026-09-26): OD-C8, OD-E1 / OD-E9 (simulation only), OD-E2 = (b), OD-E4. Still open, with the recommended default adopted unless you object: OD-B13, OD-D11, OD-D12, OD-E3, OD-E5–OD-E8; everything else is decided or adopted. The final section lists every decision point with its question, options and status.

| D-G5 | One gene-ID resolver and control registry; exact-case species test against both species' gene tables; ≥ 95% resolution or `refused` | ag7's silent 0/498 root markers (`data/mouse_ag7/out_fin/finish_log.md:47`); MERSCOPE symbols only; `CONTROL_TOKENS` (`clustering_squidpy.py:62-69`) lacks "intergenic" | H/M |
| D-G6 | The M10 simulator and its isotonic local-threshold step with composition reweighting move into v1 (M3b, M4); calibrated-probability output, measured contamination and platform factors stay v1.1 (OD-E2, decided 2026-09-26: (b)) | §8.9 | M/L |
| D-G7 | Panels > 1,000 genes: family bundles on `/srv/storage` with their reference markers kept (subsets need only query markers); per-parent marker prefilter; PREP memory from an M3b measurement (OD-E8); `--n_processors` / `--max_gb` explicit; whole-WHB refused | WMB query markers peak RSS 20.2 / 27.1 GB at 500 / 815 genes (~22 MB per gene → ~120–170 GB at 5K [L]); reference markers RSS 4.7 GB (capped by `--max_gb 60`), h5 5.9 / 9.4 GB (linear in genes); wall 50 vs 19 min for 500 vs 815 genes, so time follows host load and the 14.06M taxon pairs, not genes (16 processes; `research/selfmap/panel_ref/*.log`); whole-WHB 4.4 h at 296 genes | M/L |
| D-G8 | (rev3) New panels are validated by simulation only (OD-E1 / OD-E9, decided 2026-09-26): gate P = pre-registered simulation criteria NP1–NP9 (held-out Allen cells restricted to the panel, thinned, perturbed, mapped with the production configuration; frozen-threshold replicates over held-out donors or test draws and seeds; stress recipes; scored per (level, class) on sets of ≥ 200 confident calls and dry-run on the seeded panels), with stricter margins kept in production; no public validation downloads; real in-house datasets get label-free QC that can only downgrade or warn (§8.8, §14) | E2 verdict 1 (the recipe met the targets in 30/30 validated combinations) and verdict 5 (native low-count cells carry 30–45% more genes than thinned; precision optimistic by up to 5–10 points below ~60 counts on Xenium); E2 caveat 1: errors that persist at full depth (contamination, reference or AD-state mismatch) escape its self-referential checks; seed noise 1.6–3.0% of WMB subclass labels (E7) | L |

### Pipeline

| # | Decision | Evidence | Conf. |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-P1 | New CPU main-env processes `ANNOTATE_PANEL`, `ANNOTATE_REFERENCE_PREP` (no `storeDir`), `CLUSTERING_SQUIDPY_ANNOTATE_MAP`, `_ANNOTATE_RESOLVE`, `_COMPUTE_CPU`, `ANNOTATION_REPORT` | Wiring map §e.2 Option A; R-eng M3, M5 | H |
| D-P2 | Legacy script text byte-identical (pinning test); shadow evaluation is a standalone CLI | R-eng M1, R-fea | H |
| D-P3 | Mode resolved per species; independent flips | R-fea | H |
| D-P4 | MAPMYCELLS disabled in map_first; deprecated after the flip | R-eng M4 (`main.nf:1628-1632`, `814-850`) | H |
| D-P5 | (OD-A3, decided.) Snapshot + `_mapfirst` key until each species flips | FINALIZE rewrites in place; `results/P7513` = 264 GB | M |
| D-P6 | (OD-D5, decided.) One integration branch; M0 (and this plan, M0-docs) on `main`; gate merges (§2.2) | CI runs on PRs into any base (`ci.yml` `pull_request:`) [H] | H |
| D-P7 | (OD-D6, decided.) New code in new files; `main.nf`, `config.py`, `io/samplesheet.py` only at hook points (§2.4) | GASTON rewrites the terminal stage, adds a fifth table-key copy, appends to `config.py` / `samplesheet.py` [H] | H |

## 2. Rollout, branches and repo conventions

### 2.1 Staged rollout

| Phase | Milestones | User-visible result | Gate |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0. Protect | M-1: step 1 (archive) done 2026-09-26; steps 2–4 pending (operator, about 0.5 day) | SSD1 cache and legacy outputs snapshotted | – |
| A. Integrity (on `main`) | M0a–M0e (4–6 days; M0a in parallel, not blocking) | Safe cache, reproducible envs, recorded Leiden engine, VZG2 fix, Ensembl IDs | – |
| B. Human MVP + generality | M1 → M2 → M3 → **M3b** → M4 → M5 → M7 (human report items) → **M8** (about 33–45 days after M0b/c/e) | M3: `merxen annotate` labels for published results. M3b: any panel gets diagnostics, resolvability, trust state. M8: `map_first` human default | **H**: §14 human criteria + H18 |
| C. Mouse MVP | M6 (after M3b, M4) → **M6b** (AP axis) → **M9**, which also accepts the mouse report items built in M7 (+9.5–13 days and ~2 days compute; can overlap M5–M8 with a second developer) | `map_first` mouse default | **M**: §14 mouse criteria incl. MO10 |
| D. New panels | **M13** (simulation programme after M3b and M4; downgrade-only real-data QC wiring after M6, M6b (mouse G4 windows / AP estimate) and M7; parallel with M5–M9; 7–10 days + CPU compute), then per panel family | A panel family moves `provisional` → `validated` by simulation (OD-E1) | **P**: simulation criteria NP1–NP9 per family |
| E. v1.1 | M10 (LL, calibrated probabilities, measured recipe, gate v2), M11 (opt-in de novo split) | Calibrated probabilities; exploratory subclusters | **1.1**: re-acceptance |
| F. Cleanup | M12 (one release after both flips) | Legacy removed | OD-D1, OD-D2 |

Effort estimates are [L] with about 30% contingency (the draft's M4 and M7 were judged 1.5–2× under-estimated, R-fea). Path sums: M0b/c/e 2–3; M1 2.5–3.5; M2 4–5; M3 3–4; M3b 7.5–10; M4 3.5–5; M5 5–6; M7 4–6; M8 3–5 (human 35–48 incl. M0); M6 3–4; M6b 4–5; M9 2.5–4 (mouse 9.5–13); M13 7–10 in parallel (rev2: 7–9). If OD-B13 fires, LL moves before M8 (+4–6 days). Threshold renegotiation at a gate: PI + implementer, time-boxed to 2 days, recorded in the gate PR.

### 2.2 Branches and PRs (OD-D5, decided 2026-09-26)

**Model.**
- **Integration branch** `feature/robust-celltype-annotation`, cut from `main` after the M0-docs PR (this plan + the convention notes, below) merges; it holds all rca work except M0 and plan revisions.
- **Milestone sub-branches** `feature/rca-m<N>-<slug>` from the integration head; one PR each into the integration branch; CI (the `pull_request:` trigger already covers any base) and your review; merge commit (prefixed commits kept); sub-branch deleted. A sub-branch merges the integration head before its PR (merge once pushed; rebase only while local).
- **M0 exception:** M0a–M0e go to `main` as `bugfix/rca-m0<x>-<slug>` (M0c `chore/rca-m0c-env`); after each, `main` is merged into the integration branch.
- **Keeping current:** merge `main` into the integration branch (never rebase it; it is shared and long-lived) after every M0 PR; after any `main` commit touching `workflows/{main.nf,nextflow.config,conf/dwight.config}`, `src/merxen/{config.py,io/samplesheet.py}` or `src/merxen/analysis/{clustering_squidpy,mapmycells,mender}.py`; at least weekly; before any gate PR. Each merge runs `pytest` and `nf-metro validate` before push.
- **CI and protection** (M1 `[chore]`): add the integration branch to `on.push.branches` in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` and an `nf-metro validate assets/metro_map.mmd` step (installed in the job; today it runs only in the local pre-push hook, and only when installed); protect the branch like `main` (PR + green CI).

**What merges to `main`, and when.**

| Gate | Trigger | Integration → `main` PR content | Version |
|---|---|---|---|
| **H** | §14 human flip rule + H18 (M8) | Everything on the branch: M1–M5, M3b, M7, the M8 flip commit; mouse code (M6, and M6b / M13 if done) merges inert with the mouse default still `legacy`. Requires CI green, dwight slow tests green, legacy pin unchanged, acceptance summary linked | next minor (0.2.0 if first) |
| **M** | §14 mouse flip rule (M9) | M6b if not yet on `main`, the M9 flip commit, anything merged since H | next minor (0.3.0 if second) |
| **P** | simulation criteria NP1–NP9 for a panel family (OD-E1) | the `validated_panels.csv` row (`validation_basis = simulation`) and its `validated_panel_levels.csv` rows + the simulation evidence. Before the first species gate has merged, `main` has neither that file nor the trust-state code, so gate-P PRs go into the integration branch; afterwards they go straight to `main` as their own PR | patch or minor |
| **1.1** | re-acceptance of H1–H3, H6, H7, H9 (M10) | M10; M11 (default off) | next minor |
| **12** | one release after both flips | M12 | 1.0.0 |

Gates may pass in either order; a species that has not passed keeps `legacy` as default in the merged code. If gate M passes first, its PR carries everything on the branch with the human default still `legacy`, and gate H later carries the human flip and anything merged since. Opt-in work with unchanged defaults (M11, `annotation-panel-simulate`) may ride along with any gate merge. After each gate, `main` is merged back and work continues on the same branch (mouse after H, then v1.1); the branch is deleted after gate 12 or when you close the effort.

**Convention note** (M0-docs: one `[docs]` PR straight to `main` that also commits this plan as `docs/plans/robust-celltype-annotation-plan.md`, so both notes on `main` point at a file `main` has; the integration branch is cut after it merges; later plan revisions go to `main` as small `[docs]` PRs and are merged into the integration branch like any `main` commit):
- `CLAUDE.md` › Workflow: "Exception: the robust cell-type annotation effort (`docs/plans/robust-celltype-annotation-plan.md`) uses the long-lived integration branch `feature/robust-celltype-annotation`. Milestone branches `feature/rca-m<N>-<slug>` are cut from it and merged into it by PR. Keep it current by merging `main` into it; never rebase it. Only acceptance-gate PRs merge it into `main`. Data-integrity bugfixes still go to `main` first and are then merged into it."
- `Agents.md` › Git Workflow › Branches (after the line "When using branches, branch from `main`, keep them short-lived …", `Agents.md:227`): "Exception: a long-lived integration branch is allowed only when a plan in `docs/plans/` names it; it follows the same PR, CI and commit-prefix rules and is deleted when the effort closes. Milestone branches may be cut from it and merged back into it by PR, and are deleted after merge."

**Unchanged rules.**
- **No milestone may change legacy results.** Legacy stays the default until gates H (human) and M (mouse); a pinning test (§13.2) keeps the legacy Nextflow script text byte-identical, so merging never invalidates legacy `-resume`. Deliberate exceptions, documented in their PRs: M0b (no extended JSON or logs in `uns`), M0e (4 genes gain Ensembl IDs in the MMC query), M0d (new provenance scalars).
- **Prefixes** (Agents.md): `[bugfix]:` M0; `[refactor]:` table keys and cell-set helper; `[feature]:` new flagged behaviour and the flips (breaking); `[minor]:` small default changes (e.g. `mapmycells_n_processors`); `[test]:`, `[docs]:`; `[chore]:` env, lockfile, CI, metro SVG.
- **Before each commit** `ruff check . --fix`, `ruff format .`, `mypy src/`; **before each push** the pre-push hook `scripts/run_ci_checks.sh` (`pytest -m "not slow"`, and `nf-metro validate` when `nf-metro` is installed in its venv). GitHub CI (`ci.yml`) runs `ruff check .`, `ruff format --check .`, `mypy src/` and `pytest -m "not slow"` on every PR into any base, plus `nf-metro validate` from M1; real-data tests carry `@pytest.mark.slow`.
- **Code standards:** Google-style docstrings and type hints on public functions; pydantic or dataclasses; `logging`, never `print`; no file over 500 KB or data in the repo; dependencies only via `pyproject.toml` + `uv pip compile pyproject.toml --extra dev -o requirements/requirements.lock`.
- **Deprecation warnings** only after that species' flip (R-eng).
- **Every milestone adding a Nextflow process** updates `assets/metro_map.mmd`, re-renders `docs/images/merxen_metro_map*.svg` (`scripts/render_metro_map.py`) and keeps `tests/test_workflows/test_metro_map.py` green (width ≤ 2000, aspect ≤ 3, no wrapped labels); an unmapped process needs a temporary `OMITTED_PROCESSES` entry (R-eng X1).
- **`.env.example`** gains `MERXEN_TEST_DATA_DIR` and `MERXEN_TEST_REFERENCE_STORE` (documented in `docs/development.md`).

### 2.3 M-1: protective actions (operator)

1. **Evidence archive — done 2026-09-26:** `$S` copied in full to `$A` (6,065 files, 38 GB), `$A/MANIFEST.sha256` verified, `/tmp` copy removed; all exit criteria refer to `$A`. (History: `/tmp` is cleaned at boot and after 30 days; the root filesystem was 90–95% full.)
2. **SSD1 cache** (OD-A2, adopted; pending): read-only snapshot of `/media/mathieubo/SSD1/MerXen/mapmycells/references/` (at least `region_frontal_a44_a45_a46_a32_acc/`); tell collaborators not to run `--mapmycells_reference_mode region|both` on dwight until M0b merges — the frontal manifest already mismatches, so the next region run would delete `precompute/`, `reference_markers/` and `query_markers/` (wiring map §0.5).
3. **Legacy outputs** (OD-A3, decided; pending): before any `map_first` pipeline run, snapshot to `/srv/storage/MerXen/legacy_snapshot_<date>/`: `results/<pair>/<seg>/clustering_squidpy/` (about 1.7 GB per pair × segmentation), the zarr tables `table_*_clustering_squidpy`, and the MENDER and cortical-depth outputs.
4. **Samplesheet check:** `workflows/samplesheet.clustering_recovery.csv` line 5 (P5011) points to `/media/mathieumbo/SS3/...` (`mathieumbo` for `mathieubo`); fix in M0b if the sheet is used [H typo; impact unknown].

### 2.4 Hook points and coexistence with `feature/gaston` (OD-D6, decided 2026-09-26)

New behaviour lives in new files. The three files GASTON also edits change only at hook points marked `// rca-hook:H<n>` / `# rca-hook:H<n>`; `test_rca_hooks.py` asserts each marker occurs exactly once, so a rebase cannot silently drop one.

| Hook | Place | Change | Size [L] |
|---|---|---|---|
| H1 | `main.nf` includes | `modules/annotation.nf`, `subworkflows/clustering_map_first.nf` | 3 lines |
| H2 | `main.nf` `clusteredSpatialdataTableKey` (`:408-415`) | New signature `clusteredSpatialdataTableKey(sourceTableKey, segmentation, suffix = "")`; the suffix (`_<suffix>` when non-empty) is applied on all three return paths (reseg / `table_MOSAIK_proseg`, original_seg / `table_original`, default); callers (MENDER, cortical depth, distance) pass `settings.clustering_squidpy_table_key_suffix` | ~12 |
| H3 | `main.nf` `rowSampleSettings` (`:1408`; map at `:1725`) | `settings += AnnotationSettings.forRow(row, params, species)`; the `runMapMyCells` override (§3.1) | ~8 |
| H4 | `main.nf` `appendClusteringSquidpyPreflightChecks` (`:910-995`) | legacy checks under `if (mode == "legacy")`; `AnnotationPreflight.append(errors, settings, params)` | ~6 |
| H5 | `main.nf` workflow block, PREPARE → COMPUTE | legacy wiring unchanged; `map_first` calls `CLUSTERING_MAP_FIRST`, which emits FINALIZE's input shape | ~25 |
| H6 | `main.nf`: a **new** `workflow.onComplete` block (none exists in `main.nf` or `nextflow.config` today) | failed-annotation / refused-panel summary via `AnnotationSettings.completionSummary()` | ~5 |
| H7 | `nextflow.config`; `conf/dwight.config` | `includeConfig 'conf/annotation.config'`; inside the dwight profile `includeConfig 'dwight.annotation.config'`. These files hold annotation-only params; params introduced by M0 live in `nextflow.config` / `dwight.config` on `main` (§3.7), and `test_annotation_config_no_duplicates.py` fails if any param is defined in both, so an include can never silently override a profile value | 2 |
| H8 | `src/merxen/config.py` | `ClusteringSquidpyConfig` +6 fields, `ClusteringSquidpySampleConfig` +2, `MenderConfig` +1; models imported from `merxen.annotation.config` | ~20 |
| H9 | `src/merxen/io/samplesheet.py` | `anatomical_region`, `mouse_section_regions` via `merxen.annotation.samplesheet_columns.parse_optional_columns(row)`; 2 `SamplePair` fields | ~10 |

**Conflict hotspots for the GASTON rebase** (`git diff main...feature/gaston`) [H]:
1. `currentPairTerminalStage` / `currentPairTerminalExpectedCount`: GASTON replaces `mender_bypass_terminal_gate` with `gaston_bypass_terminal_gate` and adds `extraClustering` to the counts (its base predates `main`'s MENDER bypass). rca does not edit them but changes their input (`run_mapmycells = false` in map_first).
2. Table key: GASTON adds `clusteredAnalysisTableKey(platform, segmentation)`, a fifth copy of `"${sourceTableKey}_clustering_squidpy"`, next to `analysisLayerKeys`; rca adds the suffix (H2) and a sync test that fails while any extra copy exists.
3. `rowSampleSettings` settings map (H3; GASTON adds ~186 lines there). 4. Preflight: GASTON's `appendGastonPreflightChecks` + a call in `runPreflightChecks` vs H4. 5. Stage functions (`normalizeStage`, `activeStageOrder`, `validateStage`): GASTON adds `gaston`; rca adds no stage (no textual conflict; stage-order tests change). 6. Workflow block, MENDER / terminal events (`main` `:3590-3731`; GASTON `:3627-3733` + a 393-line tail) vs H5. 7. `nextflow.config`, `dwight.config`: both append. 8. `config.py`: `GastonConfig` right after `ClusteringSquidpyConfig` (`:1042`) vs H8. 9. `io/samplesheet.py` and `tests/test_io/test_samplesheet.py` (H9). 10. Shared tests and docs: `test_analysis_segmentation_branches.py`, `test_dwight_profile.py`; `docs/{configuration,outputs,pipeline,samplesheet,python-api,running-the-pipeline}.md`, `README.md`.
11. Semantics: GASTON writes `gaston_*` into the clustered SpatialData table (`write_spatialdata_table: true`), so it must use the suffixed key and run after FINALIZE; GASTON adds a process without a metro station.
12. Clustering completion barrier and `analysis_layer_segmentations`: GASTON rewrites `clustering_done_per_pair_ch` (adding `clustering_completion_expectations_ch` and `groupKey(pairId, required_clustering_segmentations.size())`) directly after PREPARE / COMPUTE / FINALIZE (`main` `:3318-3326`; GASTON hunk `@@ -2756,12 +3246,27 @@`), and splits `analysis_segmentations` from `analysis_layer_segmentations` in about 15 QC, MECR, visualize and spatial-gene hunks. H5 and ANNOTATION_REPORT's wait on cortical depth sit in this region.

**Rebase guide for the GASTON author.**
1. Rebase GASTON onto current `main` first (58 commits, independent of rca), then onto `main` after gate H: `git fetch origin && git rebase origin/main` (a private feature branch, so rebasing is fine).
2. Resolve: keep `main`'s `mender_bypass_terminal_gate` and add GASTON's flag as an extra early return, re-derive the expected counts, run `test_mender_module.py` and `test_gaston_stage.py` under both clustering modes; delete `clusteredAnalysisTableKey(platform, segmentation)` and call `clusteredSpatialdataTableKey(analysisLayerKeys(platform, segmentation).table_key, segmentation, settings.clustering_squidpy_table_key_suffix)` (Python: `merxen.table_keys.clustered_table_key(source_table_key, segmentation, suffix)` for `GastonConfig.clustered_table_key`) so `test_table_keys_sync.py` passes; keep GASTON's `groupKey` completion barrier (hotspot 12) and feed it from FINALIZE's output, which both clustering modes emit (`CLUSTERING_MAP_FIRST` ends in the same FINALIZE); keep both sides of settings, preflight, params, `config.py` and samplesheet (moving GASTON params to `conf/gaston.config` is optional); do not change the legacy clustering heredocs (`test_legacy_script_pin.py`), or update the pin in a separate commit with a reason; add a `gaston` metro station or an `OMITTED_PROCESSES` entry.
3. Run `pytest`, `nf-metro validate` and a stub run with `--gaston_enabled true` under `clustering_squidpy_mode_human = legacy` and `map_first`.
4. GASTON reads neither `broad_class`, `leiden` nor `hierarchical_cluster` (grep of `gaston.py`) [H]: only the table key and the terminal barrier affect it.

## 3. Target architecture

### 3.1 Process graph (map_first mode; legacy mode unchanged)

```
analysis_samples_ch (pair, seg, samples_json, settings)
 └► CLUSTERING_SQUIDPY_PREPARE           (unchanged script text; CPU, main env)
      │  prepared/<plat>/<sid>_prepared.h5ad, manifest.json, clustering_squidpy_config.json
      ├► ANNOTATE_PANEL                   (CPU, 1 cpu / 4 GB, seconds; per pair×seg)
      │     gene-ID resolution + controls (§8.4) → panel_report.json
      │     panel_genes.json per annotation panel (intersection or per platform, §8.5)
      │     [+ panel_genes_setc.json for same-panel human pairs], panel_hash (declared panel), panel family
      │     required_bundles.json: list of (reference_id, panel_hash) this pair×seg needs
      │     (or params.annotation_panel_genes_path, for prepare-only runs)
      │        │ flatMap required_bundles → (species, reference_id, panel_hash, panel file) ; .unique()
      │        ▼
      │   ANNOTATE_REFERENCE_PREP         (CPU, main env; 8 cpus / 64 GB / 8 h; > 1,000 genes: memory from the
      │     M3b measurement (OD-E8), 24 h; maxForks 1 on dwight)
      │     one task per unique (species, reference_id, panel_hash); ReferenceStore.get_or_build()
      │     bundle = markers + profiles + negative genes + diagnostics + resolvability + trust state
      │     → bundle_ref.json {reference_id, panel_hash, build_hash, path, panel_trust}; seconds when it exists
      │        │ each pair×seg: combine its required_bundles with the bundle_refs, then
      │        │ groupTuple(by: [pair, seg]) with groupKey(size = that pair×seg's required count)
      ▼        ▼  (per_platform pairs need 5 bundles: 2 panels × {WHB, SEA-AD} + WHB on the intersection)
 CLUSTERING_SQUIDPY_ANNOTATE_MAP          (CPU, main env; 6 cpus / 24 GB, 48 GB if > 1,000 genes; per pair×seg)
      │  MMC per reference (+ set-c or intersection runs; + mouse region inference and pruned re-map)
      │  → annotation_out/<plat>/<sid>_mmc_<reference_id>.parquet, map_manifest.json
      ▼
 CLUSTERING_SQUIDPY_ANNOTATE_RESOLVE      (CPU, main env; 2 cpus / 16 GB; minutes)
      │  resolvability-gated emission, thresholds, floors, gates, consensus, statuses, flags, soft vectors
      │  → annotation_out/<plat>/<sid>_celltype_labels.parquet, <sid>_annotation_manifest.json
      ▼
 CLUSTERING_SQUIDPY_COMPUTE_CPU           (CPU, main env; 8 cpus / 32 GB; no GPU lock)
      │  map-first hierarchy (branch:leaf), legacy columns, QC embedding/Leiden
      ▼
 CLUSTERING_SQUIDPY_FINALIZE              (unchanged process; table key suffix fragment, '' in legacy)
      ├─► COMPUTE_CORTICAL_DEPTH ─► DISTANCE_FROM_OBJECT_* (unchanged)
      ├─► MENDER_* (barrier unchanged; new unassigned-state policy, §4.9)
      └─► ANNOTATION_REPORT               (CPU; 4 cpus / 32 GB; waits for cortical depth when it runs)
```

MAP → RESOLVE → COMPUTE_CPU is the subworkflow `CLUSTERING_MAP_FIRST` (`workflows/subworkflows/clustering_map_first.nf`), called from hook H5 (§2.4).

**Why this shape.** Annotation needs `cell_type_mapper`, absent from the GPU env (no ctm, no SpatialData), so everything new is CPU / main env (wiring map §e.1.3). PREP is an ordinary process; the Python `ReferenceStore` manages storage (`flock`, same-filesystem temp dir, atomic rename, never auto-delete), because a `storeDir` path must resolve before `build_hash` (ctm version, file digests) is known and its move would duplicate the store across devices (R-eng M3, R-fea). Per-panel work runs once per (reference, declared panel) in PREP and is cached (§8); MAP waits only for the bundles its own pair × segmentation needs, so pairs with different panels, set-c panels and a panel with fewer references than another never stall a `groupTuple`. MAP and RESOLVE are separate, so threshold / floor / trust / flag changes re-run only RESOLVE (minutes, vs ~1–1.7 h re-mapping per pair; R-eng M5). A tiny process builds the panel from the prepared H5ADs, so PREPARE and legacy `-resume` are untouched (R-eng M1).

**Published-output reuse:** `annotation_reuse_published` (default true) skips MMC when the published `map_manifest.json` has the same query fingerprint (sha256 of `obs_names`, `total_counts`, `var_names`) and the same `build_hash` and engine parameters; dwight prunes work dirs, so no `*_prepared.h5ad` survives there (R-eng M5).

**Stage semantics:** no new `activeStageOrder` entry (the processes belong to `clustering_squidpy`) and no new `normalizeStage` aliases ("annotate" would re-run clustering and rewrite zarr tables; R-eng); annotation as its own stage is OD-D3 (adopted: no).

**MAPMYCELLS:** in `rowSampleSettings` (around `main.nf:1628`), `runMapMyCells = false` in species-resolved `map_first` unless `annotation_mode_mapmycells_stage == "legacy"` and the user names `mapmycells` in `--stop_stage` / `--only_stage`. `currentPairTerminalStage` (`main.nf:814-850`) then never returns `mapmycells`, so the MENDER `groupKey` barrier (`3646-3731`) and `mender_extra_clustering_terminal_events_ch` get the right count; `stages += ["mapmycells"]` stays before `"mender"` (`test_mender_module.py:66-72`).

**Failure semantics:** data-quality outcomes (low coverage, broad-only, failed gate, refused panel) are statuses with outputs always written; only infrastructure errors fail a task. Under the global `errorStrategy "ignore"` (`nextflow.config:450`) a failed task drops that pair × segmentation and its MENDER is never released, as today; the end-of-run summary lists failed annotations and refused or provisional panels (workflow test).

### 3.2 `ANNOTATE_PANEL` and `ANNOTATE_REFERENCE_PREP` (new `workflows/modules/annotation.nf`)

**Panel.** `merxen annotation-panel --prepared-dir … --species … --platforms …` writes `panel_report.json` (gene-ID resolution by source, unmapped features, controls removed and why, merged duplicates, species check, panel family; §8.4), one `panel_genes.json` per annotation panel (sorted Ensembl IDs, symbols, platform(s), `panel_hash` = sha256 of the sorted resolved IDs of the **declared** panel — Xenium `gene_panel.json` or the source-table `var`, the MERSCOPE codebook — never the observed or `min_cells`-filtered `var`; `panel_mode`; §8.1) and `required_bundles.json`.
- **Same-panel human pair** (Jaccard ≥ 0.9; `intersection`): E5 set a (296 genes for every current pair in the evidence; 297 after M0e if MERSCOPE H2AX resolves to Xenium's H2AFX ID — LIF, LIFR and SQSTM1 are MERSCOPE-only, the MAPT 3R / 4R probes and NRP1 Xenium-only, `data/P7513/REPORT.md:79-81`), plus `panel_genes_setc.json`. **(M2 review)** For the seeded set-a family (the 296-gene evidence hash and the post-M0e 297-gene hash) set c is set a minus the **curated E5 list of 32 genes** (`setc_exclusions_human.csv`: |median-centred log2 X / M| > 2 within confident oligodendrocytes in ≥ 3 of 4 pairs, `exp/E5/04_make_genesets.py`), one family-level panel for every pair (264 / 265 genes), because that is the set E5 validated. The label-free rule (|log2(mean X / mean M) − pair median| > 2 on pseudobulk over table cells inside the aligned shared tissue mask) cannot reproduce it: measured on the four E5 pairs it drops 44–73 genes per pair (61 on P7513, including P2RY12, GAD2, VIP, SPI1, TREM2; 22–28 at a threshold of 3; 54 label-free with ≥ 3 of 4 pairs), so it is only the flagged fallback (`label_free_rule`) for families without a curated list, and in pipeline runs it needs the ALIGN mask (refused without it). `panel_report.json` keeps the label-free result as a cross-check.
- **Pairs with different panels:** `per_platform` (§8.5). **Single-platform samples** (mouse; unpaired sections): the sample's own panel (ag7 500, VZG2 815).
- Gene IDs come from the resolver (§8.4), which generalises the M0e fix (LIF, LIFR, SQSTM1, H2AX from a *configured local* reference `gene.csv`, `annotation_gene_id_fallback_csv`). Mouse symbols map through WMB `gene.csv` (all 500 + 815 map; mouse report §7.1).

**`ReferenceStore.get_or_build(spec, panel)`** (`src/merxen/annotation/store.py`):
- `build_hash = sha256(canonical JSON)` over `schema_version`, `ANNOTATION_BUILDER_VERSION` (an integer bumped only when builder logic changes; never the git commit or package version), `reference_id` and taxonomy id, each source file's identity `{path, size, mtime_ns, sha256(first + last 64 MB)}` (full sha256 in `bundle.json`), `panel_hash`, `hierarchy`, `nodes_to_drop`, `drop_level`, `n_per_utility`, the large-panel prefilter (§8.7), the depth grid, the resolvability recipe version, ctm version and commit. **(M2 review)** Only what the bundle's content depends on: the builders' content parameters (curated asset sha256, `weak_parent_markers`, the large-panel limit) are in; the panel's symbols (bundles are ID-only and match state genes by ID, so the resolution table enters through `panel_hash`), the MAP bootstrap settings and the resolvability settings are recorded in `bundle.json` but not hashed. The resolvability recipe enters `build_hash` in M3b, with a builder-version bump, once a builder writes resolvability outputs, and then only what the self-map output depends on (recipe and version, sigma / spill parameters, `n_test_cells`, the test-cell source, the depth grid, the self-map bootstrap settings); trust and emission thresholds are applied to the cached tables outside `build_hash`. A test pins the builder code to `ANNOTATION_BUILDER_VERSION`.
- **Layout:** `<store>/<reference_id>/<build_hash>/`, built in `<store>/.tmp-<uuid>/` on the same filesystem and renamed atomically under `fcntl.flock` on `<store>/<reference_id>/.lock` (one lock per reference; also protects concurrent Nextflow launches).
- **Sources are copied with a checksum, never symlinked** — above all the SSD1 legacy precompute, which legacy code can delete (R-eng M7).
- **Nothing is deleted automatically;** `merxen annotation-store list` and `prune --unreferenced-by <results_root> --dry-run` are manual (OD-D4). SSD1 is 84% used (560 GB free); a human bundle is about 2–3 GB and a mouse bundle 1.6–2 GB for ≤ 815 genes [M]. Bundles for panels > 1,000 genes go to `annotation_reference_store_large` (dwight `/srv/storage/MerXen/annotation_references_large`, its own directory since M3b; 4.4 TB free) and keep the family's reference-marker files, so a dataset lacking some panel genes gets a subset bundle from query markers alone (~20–60 min) instead of a full rebuild (D-G7).
- **Zero-marker parents:** `validate_lookup(lookup, tree, query_genes)` drops keys for nodes absent from the tree; a parent with no marker in the query is **auto-collapsed** (children dropped; recorded in `bundle.json` and the diagnostics). The build fails only if the root has no markers; weak panels are handled by the trust state (§8.2). This replaces the `RuntimeError` at `type_assignment/marker_cache_v2.py:122-137`.

**Bundle contents:** `bundle.json` (provenance, hashes, levels, node counts, markers per parent min / median, collapsed parents, uncovered clusters, **diagnostics and trust state**); the mapping precompute (a copy) and, for mouse, a separate marker precompute; `reference_markers.h5` (≤ 1,000 genes: deleted once query markers exist, sha256 kept, rebuilt in < 1 h if needed; > 1,000 genes: kept per family in the large store), `query_markers.json`, `query_markers.filtered.json`; `profiles.parquet` (expected fraction and mean per node × panel gene from `sum`/`sumsq`/`n_cells`/`gt0`, log-normal correction validated in `exp/E1/01_stats_profile_validation.csv`; mouse from the panel precompute); `negative_genes.parquet` (§5.6); **`resolvability.parquet` + `resolvability_cells.parquet` (per simulated cell × level × depth: parent, truth, call, bp; used by RESOLVE to reweight) + `resolvability_summary.json`** (§8.3, primary references); `vocab_snapshot.csv`; `depth_grid.json`. Per reference also `region_share.parquet` (mouse) and, in v1.1, `calibration.parquet`.

**Builders** (costs for ≤ 815 genes; 5K in §8.7).

| reference_id | Role | Build recipe | Cost |
|---|---|---|---|
| `whb_frontal_supc_clus` | human primary | Verify and **copy** the SSD1 region precompute (653 leaves, 125,481 cells, all 59,357 WHB genes, so any human panel reuses it [H]), or rebuild it (`write_region_cell_metadata` + `PrecomputationABCRunner`; ROIs Human A44-A45 / A46 / A32 / ACC; `min_cells_per_leaf` 10); `truncate_precomputed_taxonomy` to SUPC→CLUS; `reference_markers --query_path <panel stub>`; `query_markers --n_per_utility 30` (`research/pilot/run_whb_region.sh`) | copy ~2 min; markers ~2 min (marker discovery 131 s); rebuild 30–60 min [M] |
| `whb_frontal_supc_clus_ho` | human, resolvability | Same ROIs without the HO donor (`annotation_calibration_holdout_donor = "auto"`: the donor with the fewest frontal cells, H19.30.002 — 32,606 of 125,481; H18.30.002 51,983, H19.30.001 40,892 — as in the pilot [H]), panel-restricted, with markers; the donor's cells (20–30k, stratified by supercluster; without the truth superclusters no call can name — sinks, Mixed/Unknown, region-implausible — as E2's `research/insilico/02_make_queries.py`; M3b review 2) are the test set (`exp/E2/ho/`, `research/insilico/01_extract.py`), topped up per non-neuronal supercluster with other-region WHB nuclei of clusters the HO training reference keeps (user decisions 2026-09-27, §8.3 step 1; `research/insilico/01b_extract_nonneurons.py`). Gate P (M13) also builds it without each of the other two donors (leave-one-donor-out, NP4) | ~18 min extraction + ~5 min [M], per panel (and per extra held-out donor in gate P) |
| `whb_frontal_supc_clus` + set c | human sensitivity | Same precompute; set-c panel stub | ~5 min per pair |
| `seaad_mr_panel` | human secondary | `precomputed_stats.SEA-AD-Multiregion.2026-06-29.h5` (207 leaves × 36,601 genes [H]) via `ensure_seaad_multiregion_inputs` (pinned URL + sha256 `9b1d5f50a412…`, `research/allen_references_mapmycells.md:199`), taxonomy metadata (0.54 GB, `:404`), panel markers n30 (`research/pilot/run_seaad.sh`) | 317 s + 126 s |
| `wmb_panel` | mouse primary | (1) ≤ 50 cells per cluster from **WMB-10Xv3 only** (13 region files), excluding the resolvability test cells, panel-restricted; (2) `precompute_stats_abc` → marker precompute; (3) `reference_markers --query_path` and (4) `query_markers --query_path` per panel, with `--n_processors` and `--max_gb` explicit; (5) copy Allen `precomputed_stats_ABC_revision_230821.h5` (1.38 GB; `n_cells` and `sum` for all 32,285 genes × 5,322 clusters [H]) as mapping precompute; (6) filter the lookup to the mapping tree, SUPT absent (`research/selfmap/build_marker_ref.py`, `run_panel_markers.sh`, `run_map2.sh`) | ~15 min + 1.5 min + 19–50 min reference markers + ~20 min query markers (peak RSS 20–27 GB), all at 16 processes [M] |
| `wmb_selfmap_testset` | mouse, resolvability | ≤ 10 WMB-10Xv3 cells per supertype (11,913 cells; 1,195 supertypes; 334 subclasses; `research/selfmap/truth.csv`) plus, for the non-neuronal classes (Astro-Epen, OPC-Oligo, Vascular, Immune), up to 30 per supertype drawn from cells the validated marker build (1) did not use, so that build is unchanged; disjoint from (1), panel-restricted | minutes |
| `wmb_region_share` | mouse | Subclass × CCF-division share from ABC MERFISH-638850-CCF `cell_metadata_with_parcellation_annotation.csv`, OB split from OLF (`exp/E7/01_merfish_tables.py`), plus AP-indexed composition windows for **all** sections (G4) | minutes |
| `whb_whole_ctx_panel` | human, optional | Whole-WHB panel markers, `nodes_to_drop` for 16 cortex-implausible superclusters, lookup filtered to the pruned tree (`research/insilico/06_wb_panel_markers.py`, `exp/E1/06_filter_ctx_lookup.py`); refused above 1,000 genes | 4.4 h on 8 processes, ~45 GB |
| `ll_whb_ctx_profiles` | human, v1.1 | 204 clusters and 15 cortex-plausible superclusters with log-normal expected counts + 7 broad contaminant profiles (`exp/E1/14_ll_contam.py`) | minutes |

### 3.3 `CLUSTERING_SQUIDPY_ANNOTATE_MAP` (per pair × segmentation)

**Inputs:** `(pair, seg, samples_json, prepared_dir, clustering_squidpy_config.json, panel files, bundle_refs)`. The config path travels in every annotation tuple because the compute CLI needs `--config` (`clustering_squidpy.nf:88-114`); annotation settings go to `annotation_config.json`, not the legacy heredoc; per-row `section_regions` / `anatomical_region` travel in `samples_json` as optional `ClusteringSquidpySampleConfig` fields.

**Per sample** (`merxen.annotation.pipeline.annotate_map`):
1. **Load** the prepared H5AD; apply the control registry (§8.4; identical to `remove_control_features` on current panels); `total_counts` / `n_genes` from the shared `merxen.clustering.cellset.select_table_cells(adata, min_counts)` (as legacy `run_scanpy_clustering`, M1); resolve gene IDs; restrict to the declared panel's genes present in the dataset and the lookup to them (`validate_lookup` with auto-collapse), recording `n_missing_panel_genes`. If a missing gene is a root marker, a parent is left with < 5 markers, or > 1% of the panel is missing, MAP requests a subset bundle through `ReferenceStore.get_or_build` (query markers from the family's kept reference markers; resolvability inherited and marked `resolvability_inherited`); > 5% missing makes the subset its own family with a full PREP (§8.1).
2. **Map table cells only** (counts ≥ `min_counts`; others get `low_counts`). A `refused` panel skips mapping; RESOLVE writes statuses only.
3. **MMC per bundle** via `mapmycells_engine.run_mmc` (a subprocess of `merxen.analysis.mapmycells_entrypoint`): `bootstrap_factor 0.5`, `bootstrap_iteration 100`, `rng_seed 0`, `n_processors = task.cpus`, `normalization raw`, `cloud_safe False`; `OMP/OPENBLAS/MKL/NUMBA_NUM_THREADS=1` inside (6 workers × 6 BLAS threads oversubscribe; R-eng); WMB with `--drop_level CCN20230722_SUPT`, the Allen mapping precompute and the panel lookup. The extended JSON goes to task-local disk and is parsed at once into a tidy parquet (per cell × level: assignment, bp, aggregate probability, avg_correlation, runner-ups 1–5 with probabilities, `directly_assigned`), then deleted (or gzipped if `annotation_keep_extended_json`). **Nothing is embedded in `uns`** (today's legacy stage makes a 652 MB P7513 H5AD that way).
4. **Human sensitivity:** same-panel pairs get a set-c WHB run (proseg_hybrid only by default, `annotation_xplat_sensitivity_segmentations`); per-platform pairs get one WHB run per platform on the intersection panel (§8.5).
5. **Mouse:** infer regions from the unpruned map (§7.2, with the M6b rule, §7.8); two-tier drop list; lookup filtered to the pruned tree; re-map only cells whose unpruned subclass was dropped (0.5–0.8%, E7) with `--nodes_to_drop`.
6. **Write** `<sid>_mmc_<reference_id>.parquet` and `map_manifest.json` (query fingerprint, bundle hashes, engine parameters, ctm version, wall time).

**Resources:** `cpus 6`, `memory 24 GB` (48 GB above 1,000 genes [L]), `maxForks params.annotation_max_forks` (2 on dwight); E5 peak PSS 2.2–3.95 GB (SEA-AD), 6.2 GB (WMB). **Environment:** asserts `importlib.metadata.version("cell_type_mapper") == params.annotation_ctm_version` ("1.7.2"); `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=""`; exports `PYTHONPATH=${projectDir}/../src` so apptainer runs use current code (R-eng).

### 3.4 `CLUSTERING_SQUIDPY_ANNOTATE_RESOLVE`

- **Inputs:** prepared H5AD counts (flags), MMC parquets, bundles (profiles, negative genes, resolvability, trust state), `annotation_config.json`.
- **Steps:** emission per (level, class, depth bin) from the bundle's resolvability tables reweighted to the dataset's soft composition, with the resulting local thresholds and the panel's floors (§8.3, §5.2–5.4); dataset gate incl. trust state (§8.2); degraded-mode consensus; statuses and final label; flags (§5.6); soft composition vectors (§5.5); mouse gate signals (§7.6).
- **Outputs:** `<sid>_celltype_labels.parquet` (§4.1); `<sid>_annotation_manifest.json` (§4.6); `<pair>_resolve_summary.json` (gate levels and warnings, trust state, realised flag rates, coverage, resolvable share per level). About 2–5 min per pair × segmentation [M].

### 3.5 `CLUSTERING_SQUIDPY_COMPUTE_CPU`

- The existing compute CLI with `--mode map_first --labels-dir …` in the main env, no GPU queue or lock; `cpus 8`, `memory 32 GB`; on dwight `maxForks params.clustering_squidpy_max_forks` (4) is added to its `withName` block, which today exists only for `CLUSTERING_SQUIDPY_COMPUTE` (R-eng).
- Builds the map-first hierarchy (§6) and whole-section QC embedding and fills legacy columns (§4.5); imports only `merxen.clustering.*`, `merxen.annotation.schema` and `merxen.annotation.vocab` (`merxen/annotation/__init__.py` stays empty). Several minutes per section [M]; for 5K panels the QC PCA uses the top 2,000 HVGs [L].
- Legacy `CLUSTERING_SQUIDPY_COMPUTE` (GPU env) is unchanged; `clustering_squidpy.py` must stay importable without ctm or SpatialData (subprocess test with a `sys.meta_path` block importing `merxen.clustering_squidpy_stages` and `merxen.clustering.*`; R-eng M6).

### 3.6 `ANNOTATION_REPORT`

Inputs per human pair × segmentation (or mouse sample): clustered H5ADs, label parquets, bundles; cortical-depth outputs (`results/<pair>/<platform>/compute_cortical_depth`, joined when `run_compute_cortical_depth` is set; the report waits for them); the shared-tissue mask (`results/<pair>/alignment/align_out/shared_tissue_mask*`) when present. Output `${outdir}/${pair}/${seg}/annotation_report/` (`report.html`, PNG + PDF figures, CSVs, `acceptance_metrics.json`). `cpus 4`, `memory 32 GB`; dwight `maxForks 2`.

### 3.7 Config and params

**Pydantic models** in `src/merxen/annotation/config.py`, imported by `src/merxen/config.py` (H8); validators are path-free because the GPU env loads the config (R-eng).
- `AnnotationReferenceSpec`: `reference_id`, `species`, `role` (primary | secondary | likelihood | sensitivity | resolvability), `sources`, `hierarchy`, `nodes_to_drop`, `drop_level`, `n_per_utility` 30, `max_cells_per_cluster` 50, `bootstrap_factor` 0.5, `bootstrap_iteration` 100, `rng_seed` 0, `depth_grid` (human ≤ 1,000 genes `[10,15,30,60,120,250]`; mouse and panels > 1,000 genes `[10,20,50,100,250,500,1000,2000]`).
- `AnnotationThresholds`: `mode` "raw" (v1) | "simulation_calibrated" (v1.1); raw defaults (validated panels; the minimum elsewhere) `whb_broad` 0.73, `whb_supercluster` 0.69, `seaad_broad` 0.68, `seaad_subclass_below60` 0.55, `seaad_subclass_from60` 0.45, `wmb_class` 0.90, `wmb_subclass` 0.80, raisable per panel (`threshold_source`); targets broad / lineage / NT / class 0.90, supercluster / subclass 0.85; provisional margins `provisional_target_margin` 0.05 (≥ 60 counts) and `provisional_target_margin_below60` 0.10, capped at `provisional_target_cap` 0.97, and `provisional_mouse_subclass_floor` 60 — applied to `provisional` panels and to families validated by simulation (`validation_basis = simulation`, OD-E1); `second_vote_below_counts` 60; `floors_path` (`assets/annotation/floors_<species>.csv`); `max_leaf_level` (human `"supercluster"`, mouse `"subclass"`) **replaces `never_emit_levels`**; `allow_fine_levels` false (OD-E4, decided: report-only opt-in); `hard_min_counts` is forced equal to `ClusteringSquidpyConfig.min_counts` by a validator (R-rob).
- `AnnotationPanelConfig` (§8): `panel_mode` auto | intersection | per_platform (auto: intersection when Jaccard ≥ 0.9); `min_gene_id_resolution` 0.95; `min_mapped_genes` 50; `min_root_markers` 10; `gene_id_fallback_csv`, `gene_alias_table`, `gene_id_overrides_csv`; `control_feature_types_keep` `["Gene Expression"]`; `extra_control_patterns`; `species_exact_case_min_ratio` 0.5; `family_min_jaccard` 0.95 (same species and platform; OD-E7); `subset_bundle_missing_frac` 0.01, `own_family_missing_frac` 0.05; `large_panel_genes` 1000; `large_panel_marker_prefilter` = per-parent top-k union, cap 2000 [L] (opt-in since the M3b 5K measurement: default `"none"`, §8.7); `validated_panels_path`.
- `AnnotationResolvabilityConfig` (§8.3): `enabled` true; `recipe` "R1_contam_HO" (LogNormal(0, 0.8), spill 0.25); `n_test_cells` human 25,000, mouse 11,913 + up to 30 per non-neuronal supertype; `threshold_rule` "local_isotonic" (never set-level); `min_cells_per_bin` 50 (test cells with native counts ≥ D); `min_confident_n` 50; `wilson_margin` 0.02; `split_halves` true; `reweight_to_composition` true; `min_coverage` 0.2; `seed_stability_max_change` 0.02. Gate P (acceptance only, `scripts/acceptance/new_panel.py`, §14; rev3): `gate_p_seeds` [0, 1]; human leave-one-donor-out over the three frontal WHB donors; mouse 2 disjoint test draws, the second topping every class up to `gate_p_class_min_test_cells` where the 10Xv3 cells unused by the marker build allow (≤ `gate_p_topup_max_cluster_frac` 0.05 of any cluster's cells); `gate_p_stress` = spill 0.35, LogNormal(0, 1.0), per-gene log2 factors drawn from the measured human cross-platform offsets (capped ±2); `gate_p_min_confident_n` 200 (per tested set, pooled over donors or draws); `gate_p_replicate_min_confident_n` 100; `gate_p_class_min_test_cells` 700 (≈ 200 / 0.30; defines the class set C_P, §14); `gate_p_spread_se_multiplier` 3.5; `gate_p_min_coverage` 0.30 [L]. The rev3-check dry run on the seeded panels must pass before these values score a new family (§14).
- `AnnotationGate`: `min_frac_ge30` 0.30, `min_table_broad_coverage` 0.25, `warn_segmented_broad_coverage` 0.15, `warn_unvalidated_share` 0.10 (rev3; simulation-validated families, §8.2) [L]. `MouseGateConfig`: §7.6.
- `AnnotationRealQcConfig` (rev3; downgrade-only, §8.8): `marker_consistency_warn` (human 0.75; mouse = G2's 0.80), `marker_consistency_broad_only` 0.70 (human), `paired_broad_jsd_warn` 0.20, `uninformative_strata_warn_frac` 0.5, `genes_per_count_gap_warn` 0.45, `prefilter_spotcheck_min_agreement` 0.95 [L]; `seeded_families_warn_only_until_gate` true (§8.8).
- `AnnotationFlagsConfig`: `contamination_null` "dataset_empirical", `contamination_alpha` 0.01, `contamination_min_neg_counts` 3, `flag_rate_uninformative_above` 0.15; `diffuse_quantile` 0.95, `diffuse_rate_uninformative_above` 0.30; `ood_robust_z` −3.0; `microglial_spillover_enabled` (mouse true, human false; OD-C5), `microglia_stat_min` 10, `microglia_weight_min` 0.05, `microglia_fpr_max` 0.005, `specific_gene_ratio` 20, `specific_gene_min_share` 0.001, `min_specific_genes` 3 (§8.6); `astro_lowcount_below` 100 (mouse).
- `MouseRegionConfig` (E7 values; M6b may change starred items, §7.8): `tile_um`* 150, `min_neurons_per_tile`* 3, `min_tile_fraction`* 0.01, `min_component_tiles` 3, `min_assigned_tiles` 200; `class_low_share` 0.20, `subclass_share_in_low_class` 0.30, `subclass_share_default` 0.10, `min_merfish_cells_subclass` 20; `never_drop_classes`* Astro-Epen, OPC-Oligo, Vascular, Immune, Pineal; `coupled_regions`* (candidate `{"OB": ["OLF"]}` with AON-home defined at structure level, off until M6b); `coherence_k` 30, `coherence_min` 0.1, `coherence_max_conf` 0.8.
- `AnnotationConfig`: `enabled`, `species`, `anatomical_region` (human: only "frontal_cortex" validated; other values fail validation pointing to OD-C7 and §8.9), `references`, `panel`, `resolvability`, `thresholds`, `gate`, `mouse_gate`, `flags`, `mouse_regions`, `reuse_published` true, `keep_extended_json` false, `ctm_version` "1.7.2", `reference_store`, `reference_store_large`.
- `ClusteringSquidpyConfig` gains `mode: Literal["legacy","map_first"] = "legacy"`, `labels_dir`, `leaf_source: Literal["mapped","denovo"] = "mapped"`, `adaptive_split` (v1.1, `rule="none"`), `qc_leiden_resolution` 0.5, `table_key_suffix: str = ""`. `MenderConfig` gains `unassigned_state_policy: Literal["state","exclude_from_features"]` (§4.9).

**Nextflow params** (`workflows/conf/annotation.config`, included by H7; annotation-only, created in M1 on the integration branch):

```
annotation_xplat_sensitivity = "geneset_c"     // off | geneset_c | geneset_c+rescale (X1)
annotation_xplat_sensitivity_segmentations = "proseg_hybrid"
annotation_human_microglia_flag = false
annotation_allow_single_method = false         // degraded mode WHB-only below 60 counts (§5.3)
annotation_mouse_lkloc = false                 // optional mouse glial second opinion, report only (E3)
annotation_calibration_holdout_donor = "auto"  // HO donor (auto = H19.30.002): resolvability (v1), calibration (v1.1)
annotation_report_enabled = true
annotation_mode_mapmycells_stage = "legacy"    // "skip" after the species flip
mender_unassigned_state_policy = "exclude_from_features"   // map_first only; legacy keeps "state"
// Defined on `main` by M0, not here: mapmycells_n_processors (M0b; nextflow.config null → 4, conf/dwight.config
// keeps 8), qc_registration_strict = false (M0a), annotation_gene_id_fallback_csv = null (M0e; dwight path in dwight.config)
```

- **Species defaults** live in `workflows/lib/AnnotationDefaults.groovy` (auto-loaded from `workflows/lib/`; a `main.nf` function cannot be called from module scripts under DSL2, which is why `clustering_squidpy.nf:17-27` and `mapmycells.nf:20-21` duplicate `pipelineSpecies`; R-eng). `resolveMode(params, species)`, `tableKeySuffix(params, species)` (from a `FLIPPED` constant changed only by the flip PRs) and `forSpecies(species)` feed `main.nf` and the module heredocs; a string test compares them with the pydantic defaults, and `test_annotation_defaults.py` covers mode and suffix in all four mode × flip combinations.
- **Profiles:** `conf/dwight.annotation.config` holds the SSD1 and `/srv/storage` source paths, `annotation_reference_store = "/media/mathieubo/SSD1/MerXen/annotation_references"`, `annotation_reference_store_large = "/srv/storage/MerXen/annotation_references_large"` (M3b), and `maxForks` for PREP (1), MAP (2), COMPUTE_CPU (4), REPORT (2); the large-panel PREP `withName` variant takes `annotation_prep_large_memory`.
- **Samplesheet:** optional columns `mouse_section_regions` (auto | none | `;`-separated CCF divisions) and `anatomical_region`, parsed in `rowSampleSettings` with the global param as default, carried in `samples_json`.
- **Preflight** (`AnnotationPreflight.append`, via H4): legacy marker / taxonomy / membership checks only in legacy mode; map_first checks reference sources (or auto-download), writable stores, `annotation_ctm_version`, and override / alias paths when given.

### 3.8 Environments and containers

- **`envs/environment.yml`** keeps the thin `-e "../[dev]"` and gains a header `# requirements.lock sha256: <hash>` enforced by `tests/test_workflows/test_env_lock_sync.py`, so any lockfile change forces Nextflow to rebuild `work/conda/env-*`; the runtime ctm assertion (§3.3) is the second line of defence. Installing from the lock fails with pip `ResolutionImpossible` (the ctm direct URL `@v1.7.2` differs from `@824caef…`, and ctm's own `abc_atlas_access @ git+…` has no ref; verified with pip 25.0.1 in `review_engineering/piptest/`; R-eng B1). Full pinning via a prebuilt env: OD-D7 (adopted: thin + header).
- **ctm lock pin verified:** `824caef975618afdadf31172fe2d57e61e657b92` is the dereferenced v1.7.2 tag [H].
- **`containers/Dockerfile`** installs from the lock with uv (`uv pip install -r requirements/requirements.lock && uv pip install --no-deps .`); rebuilt at M0c and at each flip. The new processes export `PYTHONPATH`, so no rebuild per merge. The apptainer `-B /data,/nfsdata` binds miss `/media` and `/srv/storage`; document binds for both stores (OD-D8).
- **`pyproject.toml`:** add `scikit-learn>=1.3,<2` (logistic and isotonic regression; only transitive today); package data `assets/annotation/*.csv` and `*.yaml`; no other runtime dependency (no rctd-py, RESOLUTE or scvi); regenerate the lock.
- **GPU env:** unchanged; only legacy COMPUTE uses it (OD-D2).
