**ID resolution** (first hit wins; the source is recorded): (1) native Ensembl in `var` (`ensembl_id`, `gene_ids`, `gene_id`, `feature_id`; `merxen.gene_ids.is_ensembl_gene_id`; version suffix stripped) — Xenium v1 and 5K provide these; (2) the pair lookup `collect_gene_id_lookup_for_samples` (today copies Xenium IDs onto the MERSCOPE side; `clustering_squidpy.py:2748`); (3) the primary reference's local `gene.csv`, casefolded symbol match, as `_supplement_ensembl_id_column` does (`mapmycells.py:282-315`) — the generalised M0e fallback and the main path for symbol-only MERSCOPE panels; (4) an optional local alias table (HGNC / MGI previous and alias symbols; single-target aliases only; no network in tasks); (5) curated overrides (`gene_id_overrides_<species>.csv`, each with a reason).

**Checks:** *Species* — before the case-insensitive fallback, the exact-case match rates of the panel symbols are computed against both local gene tables (WHB and WMB `gene.csv`; HGNC symbols are upper-case, MGI symbols such as `Gfap` are not); `refused` (`species_mismatch`) if the other species matches exact-case better, or if the run species' exact-case matches are < 50% of its case-insensitive matches. Without this, a mouse MERSCOPE panel run under the default `species = "human"` (`nextflow.config:6`; one species per run, `main.nf:1754`) would resolve `Gfap` to human `GFAP` through the casefolded lookup (`mapmycells.py:253-259`, `:294-302`) and pass the prefix check by construction. Native IDs must carry the run's prefix (ENSG / ENSMUSG, ≥ 95%), else `refused`. Release drift: a native ID absent from the reference whose symbol is present takes the reference's ID (`id_resolution = symbol_fallback`). Duplicates resolving to one ID are summed and recorded. Genes absent from a reference (reporters / transgenes such as GFP or tdTomato; isoform add-ons such as `Snap25a` / `Snap25b` in the public 10x 5K pup data; genes missing from SEA-AD's 36,601) stay in the H5AD, are excluded from that reference's mapping, and are listed. The ag7 0/498 failure mode (`data/mouse_ag7/out_fin/finish_log.md:47`) becomes a loud `refused`. `panel_hash` covers the resolved IDs of the declared panel (§8.1); the resolution table's sha256 enters `build_hash`. *M3b deviation:* bundles stay keyed by the resolved IDs; the declared panels' resolution sha256 and gene tables are recorded in `bundle.json` (`built_from_panel.declared_resolutions`), not hashed, so two resolutions giving the same IDs share one bundle.

*Observed in passing (outside this effort):* `qc/gene_comparison.apply_dataset_filter` drops `Blank` for XENIUM and Xenium codeword names for MERSCOPE (`qc/gene_comparison.py:101-114`); the pairing looks swapped [M] — a separate `[bugfix]` to `main` (OD-D12).

### 8.5 Pairs with different panels (`panel_mode`)

- `auto` (default): Jaccard of the two platform panels ≥ 0.9 → `intersection` (today's set a + set c); otherwise `per_platform`.
- `per_platform`: each platform is annotated on its own resolved panel (own resolvability, trust, thresholds, floors), plus one WHB run per platform on the intersection panel (`mmc_whb_xpanel_*`) that feeds every cross-platform statement (composition JSD, density correlation, gene factors, per-label pseudobulk r). If the intersection has < 100 genes or is broad-only by resolvability, cross-platform statistics are broad-level only and flagged [L].
- Unpaired sections use their own panel; set c exists only for same-panel pairs. PREP runs once per unique (species, reference, panel); MAP collects each pair × segmentation's required bundles (§3.1).

### 8.6 Mouse on Xenium, and gene sets derived per panel

- **Floors and thresholds:** the unknown-panel rules (§5.4, §8.3), also after validation by simulation (OD-E1).
- **G1:** the density ratio is platform-agnostic; the shift test uses the platform's own cells (Xenium Onboard Analysis cells; Vizgen boundaries on MERSCOPE). The 1.5 / 2.0 thresholds were set on MERSCOPE VZG2 and are re-checked on the first in-house Xenium mouse section (no public 5K data, OD-E1) [L].
- **Spill-over genes:** the E3 rule already derives the set from the reference (`exp/E3/09b_specific_flag.py`: microglia mean / max non-target class mean ≥ 20 and share ≥ 1/1000, immediate-early genes excluded); needs ≥ 3 genes, else null with reason `insufficient_panel_genes`; held-out validation needs ≥ 6 (build on half, test on half); the FPR check uses the panel's derived astrocyte set, not Aqp4 alone.
- **G2 referee:** class marker sets per panel from the same specificity rule, not hand lists [L]. **G4:** MERFISH is MERSCOPE-type; human Xenium reads astrocytes high (33.1% vs 23.4%, D-A7), so mouse Xenium Astro-Epen may warn for platform reasons (provisional bands) [L]. **Region inference** is platform-agnostic.

### 8.7 Compute, memory and caching for large (5K) panels

| Step | ≤ 815 genes (measured) | 5K (estimate) |
|---|---|---|
| WHB frontal reference markers | 131 s marker discovery at 296 genes (`research/pilot/run_whb_region.log`) | ~10–60 min [L] |
| SEA-AD MR reference markers | 242 s (`run_seaad.log`) | ~70 min [L] |
| WMB panel extraction (240k cells, 13 files) | ~15 min | 15–30 min (I/O-bound) [L] |
| WMB reference markers | ag7 (500 genes): 50 min wall, 27,430 user-s, peak RSS 4.7 GB, h5 5.9 GB; VZG2 (815): 19 min, 15,756 user-s, RSS 4.7 GB, h5 9.4 GB; both `--n_processors 16 --max_gb 60`, 14.06M taxon pairs (`research/selfmap/panel_ref/refmarkers_*.log`) | h5 ~60 GB (~24 GB with the prefilter; size is linear in genes) [L]; wall time follows host load and the taxon-pair count, not genes: a wide 0.5–8 h [L]; RSS bounded by `--max_gb` |
| WMB query markers | ag7 19.5 min, peak RSS **20.2 GB**; VZG2 20.2 min, peak RSS **27.1 GB**; 16 processes (`querymarkers_*.log`) | RSS grows ~22 MB per gene (it loads the reference-marker array and copies it for the largest parents): ~53–66 GB with the 2,000-gene prefilter, ~120–170 GB at 5K [L]; 20–60 min [L]. **Binding step for 5K PREP memory** |
| PREP memory reserve | 64 GB covers ≤ 815 genes (largest step 27 GB) | > 1,000 genes: the M3b measurement at 8 processes + 30% (probably 128–192 GB, `maxForks 1`), or the prefilter made mandatory (OD-E8) [L]. *Measured (M3b): peak 37.7 GB (21.3 GB without the prefilter) + 30% = 49 GB → 64 GB* |
| Resolvability | human ~35 min, mouse ~25 min [M] | 1–2 h [L] |
| MMC mapping | WHB 13.2–13.6, WMB 41.7 processor-s per 1k cells (E5) | 1–3× [L] (markers per parent bounded by `n_per_utility`; larger query) |
| MAP memory | PSS 2.2–6.2 GB (E5) | reserve 48 GB [L] |
| Whole-WHB optional bundle | 4.4 h, ~45 GB | refused (~75 h extrapolated) |

**Guards above `large_panel_genes` (1,000):**
- **Measure first (M3b):** build the WMB panel precompute for the 10x Xenium Prime 5K Mouse gene list and run reference and query markers with and without the prefilter at `--n_processors 8` (all measured WMB timings used 16 processes, not the 8 budgeted); record wall time, peak RSS and disk. PREP memory for > 1,000 genes = that measurement + 30%; because it probably breaks the ≤ 40 GB shared-host etiquette, it is a decision (OD-E8). `--n_processors` and `--max_gb` are always passed explicitly. *Measured (M3b stage D, 2026-09-27; 5,006 genes, 14.06 M taxon pairs, `--max_gb 40`, `m3b/simulate/xenium_prime_5k_mouse/`):* unfiltered, reference markers 2,845 s (largest process 5.8 GB, process-tree PSS 20.9 GB, h5 14.2 GB) and query markers 1,290 s (21.3 GB, PSS 20.5 GB); prefiltered to 1,992 candidates, 2,844 s (4.9 GB, 27.6 GB, h5 12.3 GB) and 1,241 s (37.7 GB, 37.4 GB). Time and memory do not grow with the genes (the estimates above are refuted); the peak + 30% = 49 GB fits the standard 64 GB PREP reserve, so `annotation_prep_large_memory = "64 GB"` and OD-E8's shared-host exception is not needed; the production (unfiltered) 5K mouse bundle took 92 min (reference markers 2,731 s, process-tree PSS 27.2 GB) and holds 16.2 GB (13.2 GB reference markers); the 5K human bundles (WHB + SEA-AD) took 10 min.
- **Prefilter:** marker discovery on the union of the top-k genes per sibling set (per parent) in the reference precompute, capped at 2,000 — not a global between-leaf-variance ranking, which would drop genes specific to a few of the 5,303 leaves, i.e. rare-type markers. Mandatory whenever the measured query-marker RSS would exceed the reserve; recorded in `build_hash`. Validated by simulation in M3b and gate P (NP9, M13): per emitted level and per class, agreement with the unfiltered lookup ≥ 0.95 for every class with n ≥ 50 at bp ≥ 0.8, and no parent below 5 markers [L]; spot-checked on the first in-house 5K dataset (downgrade-only, §8.8). *M3b result:* version 1 (max-min greedy pair coverage per parent, k = 69 on the 5K mouse panel) keeps broad, class and NT calls (agreement ≥ 0.994 per class) but fails at subclass (0.920–0.949 in 13 of 34 classes) and saves neither memory nor time, so it is opt-in (`large_panel_marker_prefilter` default `"none"`) and mandatory only when the measured envelope (37.7 GB up to 5,006 genes, scaled beyond) exceeds the reserve.
- **Storage and caching:** PREP time 24 h; bundles in the large store (`/srv/storage`), keyed by `build_hash` (which includes the declared-panel `panel_hash`); the family's reference markers are **kept** (mapping needs only query markers, but a dataset missing some panel genes then needs only a query-marker rebuild, ~20–60 min, §3.3); resolvability inside the bundle, reused by every dataset of the family; `annotation-store list` groups by family. The precomputes already hold all genes (WHB frontal 59,357; SEA-AD 36,601; WMB means 32,285) [H], so no reference rebuild is needed; only the mouse marker precompute is re-extracted per panel.

### 8.8 Validation before a panel is trusted (OD-E1 / OD-E9, decided 2026-09-26: simulation only)

**Decision.** New panels are validated **only by in-silico simulation from the Allen references**. No public dataset is downloaded to validate a new panel: rev2's candidates (the 10x 5K mouse brain hemisphere, the Vizgen MERFISH Mouse Brain Receptor Map, Zenodo 14606776 Xenium DLPFC, the 10x Xenium Human Brain Preview, HMBA-MERSCOPE-H22.30.001) are not used, and the `--from-vendor-cells` input is dropped from v1 (§17). No public human-brain 5K dataset was found in the rev2 web search (2026-09-26) [L], and none is needed: simulation from WHB covers it. The MERFISH-638850 download (OD-C8) serves only the mouse AP-axis rule (M6b).

**Simulation (gate P; `annotation-panel-simulate --gate-p`, `scripts/acceptance/new_panel.py`; M13).** For a family's declared panel: held-out reference cells (human: the HO donor's cells, mapped against a bundle built without that donor; mouse: the self-map test cells, §8.3) are restricted to the panel, thinned binomially to the depth grid (only cells with native counts ≥ D) and, when a dataset of the family exists, weighted to its depth histogram (label-free), perturbed with R1_contam_HO (gene efficiency LogNormal(0, 0.8), 25% foreign spill) and with stress recipes, and mapped with the production configuration. Thresholds and emission are derived once (default donor or draw, seed 0), then **frozen** and re-tested on replicates: human leave-one-donor-out over the three frontal WHB donors (H19.30.002, H19.30.001, H18.30.002), mouse two disjoint test draws (the second topping every class up to 700 test cells where the pool allows), seeds 0 and 1. Precision is scored per (level, class) on the pooled held-out calls, in sets of ≥ 200 confident calls (deep bins pooled and inherited), and the spread across replicates is tested against sampling error (§14). The pre-registered criteria are NP1–NP9 (§14), dry-run on the seeded families before any new family is scored. Their margins are **stricter** than those of the real-data-validated panels (H6, H18) because simulation is optimistic: native low-count cells carry 30–45% more genes than thinned cells, and precision is optimistic by up to 5–10 points below ~60 counts on Xenium (E2 verdict 5). A family that passes keeps those margins in production (`validation_basis = simulation`, §8.2), because a new panel's real precision is never measured. Without `--gate-p`, `annotation-panel-simulate` stays the quick design aid: diagnostics and one resolvability run for any gene list (the 10x Xenium Prime 5K Human and Mouse Pan Tissue and Pathways lists, the Xenium Human Brain (266) and Mouse Brain (248) panels, a custom panel before ordering), predicting emitted levels and weak parents (mouse report §4.4 item 7).

**What simulation does not capture** (R42–R44): the native-vs-thinned gene-complexity gap (E2 verdict 5); platform effects beyond a random per-gene efficiency and foreign-class spill — measured per-platform gene factors, segmentation-driven spill-over (e.g. human Xenium reads astrocytes high, 33.1% vs 23.4%, D-A7; the microglial spill-over flag fires on 8.6% of VZG2 cells, E3) and probe behaviour; disease and age state (neurotypical WHB donors vs Braak VI donors; WMB adult mice vs the aged or lesioned ag7 tissue, OD-C4). E2's caveat 1 already notes that errors persisting at full depth (contamination, reference or AD-state mismatch) escape its self-referential real-data check; cells simulated from neurotypical references cannot show them either [L]. NP6 adds a stress draw of the measured human cross-platform gene factors (capped ±2 log2), which is only a proxy [L].

**Real in-house datasets** of every family (provisional, validated by simulation, or validated on real data) run the label-free production QC (`annotation/real_qc.py`, RESOLVE and the report). It can only **downgrade trust or raise warnings, never promote**: a passing check changes neither the trust state nor the margins. On the seeded `real_data` families (set a, ag7, VZG2), whose trust rests on gates H and M, the checks this module adds (marker referee, paired concordance, flag rates, gene complexity, prefilter spot check) only warn until that species' gate has merged (`seeded_families_warn_only_until_gate`); the dataset gates themselves apply as defined (§5.4, §7.6), and the expected warnings on the current datasets are listed in the gate PRs (H8).

| Check | Source | Effect when it fails [L] |
|---|---|---|
| Dataset gate (A, broad coverage; mouse G1–G5) | §5.4, §7.6 | As defined there (`broad_only`, `failed` or the warning flag) |
| Registration G1 (MERSCOPE and Xenium) | M0a guard, §7.6 | `failed`, `exclude_hard`, banner |
| Marker referee: canonical-marker consistency of confident broad (human) / class (mouse) calls over marker-pseudo-confident cells, per-panel marker sets (§8.6) | port of `exp/E1/09_marker_referee.py`; mouse G2 | Warning below the platform's lowest validated value (human 0.75, the H9 threshold for P1212; mouse 0.80, G2); human `broad_only` below 0.70 (mouse: G2 fails) |
| Paired-platform concordance, where a paired section exists | intersection-run soft broad JSD (§8.5) | Warning above 0.20; supercluster-level cross-platform statistics withheld for that pair |
| Flag rates | realised contamination, diffuse and spill-over rates (§5.6) | Uninformative strata reported (H16); warning when more than half of a dataset's (class × platform) strata are uninformative |
| Gene complexity | median `n_genes` of native table cells vs simulated cells per depth bin | Warning when native cells carry > 45% more genes than simulated ones (beyond E2's 30–45%) |
| 5K prefilter spot check | prefiltered vs unfiltered lookup on a 10k-cell subsample | An emitted level with agreement < 0.95 becomes `not_resolvable` for that dataset |

Per-dataset effects are automatic (gate warning flag, forced `broad_only` or `not_resolvable`, banner, a note in `<pair>_resolve_summary.json`). A failure on a dataset of a family validated by simulation also opens a review: demotion (row removed, `validated_max_level` lowered, or (level, class) rows in `validated_panel_levels.csv` set to failed) is a PR you approve. Held-out-gene enrichment (§5.8) may be run on the first in-house dataset of a new family as an extra, downgrade-only check.

### 8.9 Calibration policy for unknown platforms and panels (OD-E2, decided 2026-09-26: (b)), and other regions

**Options:** (a) promote the whole M10 calibration into v1 for panels without real-data validation; (b) **decided 2026-09-26** — the conservative v1 rule plus the HO simulator with E2's per-cell isotonic threshold step and composition reweighting: thresholds max(validated default, local t*), floors max(known, simulated), provisional margins (+0.05; +0.10 below 60 counts, capped 0.97; mouse subclass floor 60), coverage ≥ 0.2, trust states, simulation-based NP acceptance (gate P, OD-E1); (c) the conservative rule without simulation (max-over-known floors, raw thresholds).

**Why (b):** raw bp thresholds and count floors are panel-specific — bp saturates as markers grow (mouse class bp median 1.0), a 5K pan-tissue panel spreads counts over many uninformative genes, and a weak custom panel may not separate superclusters; under (c) a new panel could emit supercluster labels it cannot support or waste depth it has. The simulator is cheap (minutes per ≤ 815-gene panel) and prototyped (`research/insilico`, `exp/E2`, `research/selfmap`), and its recipe met both targets in 30/30 combinations on validated data (E2 verdict 1); the local threshold rule met its targets in every combination, the set-level rule missed in 37–50% (E2 verdict 3).

**Why not (a):** M10's remaining additions need measurements that do not exist for a new platform before its data (per-platform gene factors, measured own-fraction contamination), and calibrated probabilities improve reporting rather than safety. The 44% real-vs-simulation supercluster pass rate (R-sci) is *not* an argument against (a): it was measured for the same R1_contam_HO recipe that (b) uses, and rose to 78% after reweighting to each dataset's composition. It is an argument for taking the isotonic local-threshold step and the reweighting into v1, which (b) now does (+1–2 days in M3b, +0.5–1 in M4). Promoting the rest of M10 would add ~4–6 more days before v1 with no evidence of a further safety gain.

**Residual risk:** simulation optimism (native low-count cells carry 30–45% more genes and precision is optimistic by 5–10 points below ~60 counts, E2 verdict 5; unknown contamination), mitigated by the +0.10 margin below 60 counts (+0.05 above), floors, the SEA second vote below 60 counts (human; mouse, which has no second vote, gets the subclass floor 60 instead), the gates, the stricter gate-P simulation criteria (NP3–NP7) and downgrade-only QC on real datasets (§8.8). Because new panels are validated by simulation only (OD-E1), this optimism is never measured for them on real data, so simulation-validated families keep the margins permanently (§8.2) [L].

**Other human regions** (OD-C7, decided: frontal only, extensible): a region needs a WHB ROI set for its precompute and HO bundle, a `region_plausible_<region>` vocab column, region-appropriate held-out markers and QC (cortical depth is cortex-only), and its own acceptance; until then the `anatomical_region` validator refuses it with a pointer here.

---

## 9. QC and annotation report (`ANNOTATION_REPORT`, `annotation/report.py`)

Layout: `results/<pair>/<seg>/annotation_report/` with `report.html` (static), PNG + PDF figures, one CSV per figure, `acceptance_metrics.json`. Each item names the failure it makes visible.

1. **Annotatability, resolvability and the panel:** per platform and level, confident fraction of table cells and of segmented objects, status breakdown (incl. `not_resolvable`), resolvable share; gate values, level and warning flag; trust state and validation basis (real data / simulation), with the validated share per level and class for simulation-validated families, and a banner for `provisional` / `broad_only` / `refused`; real-data QC outcomes and any downgrade (§8.8); realised flag rates per class × platform (informative or not); **panel card** — gene-ID resolution by source, unresolved features, controls by type, markers per parent, weak and collapsed parents, nodes with < 5 panel markers, MMC "too few markers" warnings, resolvability precision-coverage curves per level and depth, D_max and the `resolvability_extrapolated` share per class, missing declared-panel genes, thresholds (unweighted and reweighted) and floors with their source; self-thinning diagnostic where ≥ 500 cells have ≥ 200 counts, stating its truth composition (P1212_M's 509 such cells are 89% Vascular → labelled unreliable). *Catches:* broad-only datasets, silent degradation, over-firing flags, unsupported levels, ID mismatches.
2. **Composition per level:** soft (headline) with 95% block-bootstrap CI; confident-only; argmax; whole section vs shared mask; depth-stratified; `unallocated` and `Mixed/Unknown` by reason; sinks and implausible nodes highlighted; COP-derived OPC share. *Catches:* abstention bias, COP / CGE / Splatter sinks.
3. **Confidence vs counts:** 2D histograms of raw bp, avg_correlation and thresholds vs `total_counts` per platform (v1.1 adds calibration curves). *Catches:* flat bp (P1212, P5011), saturation (mouse, large panels).
4. **Reference-expectation dotplots:** per confident label, observed fraction-positive and mean on panel genes next to `profiles.parquet`; pseudobulk–centroid r and pseudobulk re-mapping; genes chosen per platform and panel (top specific genes for large panels). *Catches:* Xenium "Microglia" lacking P2RY12; astrocyte spill into neurons.
5. **Negative-marker purity:** `contamination_score` per label and platform, flag rate. *Catches:* AQP4 / GJA1 in 66–74% of Xenium Exc cells (P7513 F3).
6. **Method agreement:** WHB vs SEA (v1.1 + LL) at lineage and 7 classes by depth quartile; tier fractions and spatial tier map; the coverage cost of the below-60 rule.
7. **Cross-platform concordance (human):** soft broad and supercluster JSD (whole section, shared mask); per-type density correlation in 200 µm bins in the Xenium frame (`<shape>_aligned_nonrigid`); per-gene platform log2 ratios within confident labels (`<pair>_platform_gene_factors.csv`); per-label pseudobulk r; **set-c beside set a** for every composition statement (the intersection run for per-platform pairs, §8.5) and X1 when enabled.
8. **Held-out-gene enrichment summary** when the acceptance output is present.
9. **Cortical depth QC (human;** manual pia/WM GeoJSON boundaries, `src/merxen/cortical_depth/boundaries.py`, label-independent): median depth per supercluster with bootstrap CI; ordering Upper-layer IT < Deep-layer IT < Deep-layer NP/CT/6b; **at broad level for every pair incl. P1212 and P5011** oligodendrocytes WM > GM and astrocyte / neuron gradients.
10. **Mouse region plausibility:** AP estimate and bin (§7.8); inferred vs explicit regions; tile map; drop list, M6b rule variant, relabelled cells and targets; subclass × region vs MERFISH share; class composition vs the AP-matched window (G4); F1 rate; spill-over prevalence and map (not a count) with the derived gene set; Astro-Epen with and without low-count cells; gate G1–G5.
11. **AD and OOD panel (human):** per-class real / in-silico `avg_correlation` ratio and `flag_ood` rate; SEA-AD disease-supertype fraction per class vs the in-silico baseline (population level); SEA-AD glial supertypes (`calibrated=false`) when OD-B3 is on; no neurotypical composition assumption.
12. **Provenance footer:** references, hashes, ctm version, seeds, runtimes, gene set and panel family, trust state, calibration kind.

## 10. Compute budget (CPU only; 6 MMC processes; shared host, about 3× load-sensitive)

Sources: `exp/E5/E5_compute_plan.csv`, `E5_runtime_runs.csv`, E1 §7, pilot logs, `research/selfmap/run_panel_markers.log`, `panel_ref/*.log`. Host: 80 cores, 739 GB (R-fea); budget follows the ≤ 8 threads / ≤ 40 GB etiquette. Large panels: §8.7.

| Unit | Step | Basis | Estimate |
|---|---|---|---|
| Human pair (0.60–1.13 M table cells, 4 seg × 2 platforms) | WHB-frontal MMC | 13.2–13.6 processor-s per 1k cells | 29–52 min wall (proseg_hybrid 8–14) |
| | SEA-AD MR MMC | 1.9–5.4 processor-s per 1k cells | 7–30 min (proseg_hybrid 2–8) |
| | Set-c run (proseg_hybrid) or intersection runs | as WHB | 8–14 min |
| | RESOLVE (flag simulation cached per bundle) | | 2–5 min per seg [M] |
| | COMPUTE_CPU (QC embedding, Leiden) | | 3–8 min per seg [M] |
| | REPORT | | 5–10 min per seg [L] |
| | **Total, all segmentations** | | **~1.0–1.7 h wall** (heavy load ~2.5 h); proseg_hybrid only ~25–40 min |
| Mouse section (~414k cells, 4 seg) | WMB MMC | 41.7 processor-s per 1k; VZG2 original_seg (112k) 13.8 min, 6.2 GB | ~53 min; one seg ~14 min |
| | Pruned re-map, regions, gate, flags | | < 5 min |
| One-off, human per panel (≤ 815 genes) | precompute copy (or rebuild) + truncate + markers; SEA-AD markers; HO bundle + resolvability (~35 min); set-c markers per pair | | ≤ 1.5 h (≤ 2.5 h with rebuild) |
| One-off, mouse per panel (≤ 815 genes) | extraction + precompute + ref / query markers + Allen means copy + resolvability (~25 min) | | 1.5–2 h |
| One-off, 5K panel | §8.7 | | human 2–4 h; mouse 3–12 h, load-dominated [L]; memory per OD-E8. *Measured (M3b): human 10 min, mouse 92 min, peak 27 GB* |
| M3b 5K measurement | WMB panel precompute for the Xenium Prime 5K Mouse list; reference + query markers with and without the prefilter at 8 processes | | ~0.5–1 day wall [L] |
| Acceptance extras | held-out-gene re-maps (WHB, proseg_hybrid) | | +8–14 min per pair |
| M6b | MERFISH-638850 expression download (~7.6 GB, public Allen data, to `/srv/storage`; approved, OD-C8); 3 WMB panel bundles (~1.5–2 h each); 38 sections × 30k cells × 3 gene sets (~3.4M cell-maps); leave-neighbours-out region shares (minutes) | 41.7 processor-s per 1k | ~8–13 h wall on 6 processes [M rate; L load] |
| M13 | gate-P simulation per panel family (§8.8, §14): human — 2 extra leave-one-donor-out HO bundles, then 18 recipe runs (3 donors × seeds 0 / 1 base, clean at seed 0, 3 stress recipes per donor) of ~25k cells × 6 depths; mouse — a second test draw topped up to 700 test cells per class (§14), 12 recipe runs of ~12–20k cells × 8 depths; once, a dry run of the programme on set a, ag7 and VZG2; no downloads, no GPU, no re-segmentation of public data (OD-E1 / OD-E9) | 13.4 (WHB) / 41.7 (WMB) processor-s per 1k cells on 6 processes | human ~2.5–3 h, mouse ~2.5–3.5 h per ≤ 815-gene family; dry run ~8–10 h once; 5K families ~6–18 h each, load-dominated [L] |
| v1.1 | calibration: extraction 18 min + 50k cells × 2 engines | | ~1.5 h per bundle |
| M0a | VZG2 re-run (build, Cellpose-SAM under the GPU lock, ProSeg, enrich, QC, clustering) for reseg + proseg_* | not measured | [L] ~3–6 GPU-h + 6–10 CPU-h |

- **Memory:** MMC PSS 2.2–3.95 GB (SEA-AD), 6.2 GB (WMB); MAP 24 GB (48 GB above 1,000 genes); PREP 64 GB (WMB query markers peak 20–27 GB at ≤ 815 genes; > 1,000 genes per OD-E8); MAPMYCELLS reserves 160 GB today.
- **Reproducibility:** the seed alone changes 5.5% of SEA-AD subclass and 15.5% of SEA-AD supertype labels (E5; P1212 M, P7513 X) and 1.6–3.0% of WMB subclass labels (E7); bp ≥ 0.8 cells are 99.85% stable; seed and `n_processors` fixed and recorded.
- **Disk:** human bundle 2–3 GB, mouse 1.6–2 GB (≤ 815 genes; reference markers, 5.9 / 9.4 GB for ag7 / VZG2, deleted once query markers exist) [M]; a 5K mouse family bundle up to ~60 GB with its reference markers (~24 GB with the prefilter) on `/srv/storage` [L].
- **Cost avoided:** legacy MAPMYCELLS "both" mode (16 calls per pair re-reading 8.7 GB of stats; 6.5–12.3 CPU-h) is off in map_first.

## 12. Milestones (ordered, PR-sized, each mergeable with legacy defaults unchanged)

Effort in developer-days [L] with contingency. **Exit** = what must be true to merge. **Target** (§2.2): M0 PRs → `main`; every other milestone → a PR from `feature/rca-m<N>-<slug>` into `feature/robust-celltype-annotation`, which reaches `main` only at a gate. Milestones adding a Nextflow process include the metro update and workflow string tests.

### M-1: protective actions (operator; §2.3)

Step 1 (archive to `$A`, sha256 manifest) **done 2026-09-26**. **Exit (remaining, ~0.5 day):** SSD1 snapshot taken and collaborators notified; legacy outputs snapshotted before M5's first pipeline run.

### M0: data-integrity fixes (each its own PR to `main`, then merged into the integration branch)

- **M0-docs `[docs]`** (0.25 day): commits this plan as `docs/plans/robust-celltype-annotation-plan.md` and the `CLAUDE.md` / `Agents.md` branching notes (§2.2) to `main`; the integration branch is cut after it merges.
- **M0a `[bugfix]` VZG2 segmentation offset** (parallel; blocks only the use of VZG2 reseg / proseg outputs; 1–2 days + re-run).
  1. **Check current main first.** Commit `4a687f3` ("apply VZG2 manifest origin to image transforms", 2026-09-09 18:02) landed *after* the VZG2 build (spatialdata 09-08 10:04, segmentation 09-08 16:49) and changed only `_manifest_transform`; the VZG2 samplesheet passes `merscope_transform_path` (override CSV, translation (202.44, 1034.98) px = (21.86, 111.78) µm), so the build used the *override* path (R-eng).
  2. Re-run build + segment on a VZG2 crop from current main; run the registration check; root-cause only if the offset persists (candidate: `_load_transform_override` → `build_cellpose_affine_to_microns`, `cellpose.py:698-727`).
  3. Tests (`tests/test_io/test_vzg2.py`): a synthetic manifest with negative `bbox_microns` minima **and** an override CSV must put mask centroids on the synthetic transcripts; regressions: P1212, P7513 and ag7 builders keep zero offset.
  4. Guard `qc/registration.py::segmentation_registration_check` in QC per segmentation: density within 4 µm of centroids vs random points; cross-correlation shift vs the platform's own segmentation (Vizgen / XOA cells); warn at ratio < 1.5 or shift > 5 µm; fail with `qc_registration_strict` (new param, defined in `workflows/nextflow.config` on `main`); tests incl. a Xenium fixture.
  5. Check whether `task_dd3e24a8` produced work (no branch or worktree exists). 6. Re-run VZG2 reseg / proseg_* (§10). 7. **Exit:** ≥ 95% of reseg centroids within 5 µm of a Vizgen cell (24% today); density ratio ≥ 2.
- **M0b `[bugfix]` reference-cache safety and MapMyCells hygiene** (1–1.5 days; before anyone runs region / both on dwight): `prepare_region_mapmycells_reference` never runs `rmtree` (hashed dirs; legacy manifest read-only); `mapmycells_n_processors` null → 4 in `workflows/nextflow.config` (`[minor]`; no effect on dwight, where `conf/dwight.config:67` sets 8); skip `_load_abc_manifest` when files exist; no extended JSON or logs in `uns`; fix the `mathieumbo` typo if the sheet is used. Tests: update `test_prepare_region_reference_reuses_cache_and_force_rebuilds` (force-rebuild makes a new dir; the old survives); a mismatch never deletes; **update `tests/test_analysis/test_mapmycells.py:698-700`** (asserts `extended_json_text` / `stdout_log_text` / `stderr_log_text` today).
- **M0c `[chore]` environment reproducibility** (0.5–1 day): lock-hash header + `test_env_lock_sync.py` (thin `-e` stays); Dockerfile from the lock with uv; ctm version in the legacy MAPMYCELLS manifest; rebuild the apptainer image; ask before deleting stale `work/conda/env-*` with ctm 1.5.5. **Exit:** a new Nextflow env reports ctm 1.7.2.
- **M0d `[bugfix]` Leiden engine provenance** (0.5 day): record engine, iterations and seed for legacy runs; document the CPU/GPU difference; new `uns` scalars only, legacy script text unchanged.
- **M0e `[bugfix]` Ensembl IDs for LIF, LIFR, SQSTM1, H2AX** (0.5 day): fallback from the configured local WHB `gene.csv` (`annotation_gene_id_fallback_csv`, defined in `workflows/nextflow.config` with the dwight path in `conf/dwight.config`; no network), applied only in `prepare_mapmycells_query` and the annotation query builder (legacy clustering unchanged; R-eng); M3b generalises it (§8.4). Test: a MERSCOPE var lacking the four IDs. Records whether MERSCOPE H2AX resolves to Xenium's H2AFX ID (set a 296 → 297; LIF, LIFR and SQSTM1 are MERSCOPE-only and do not enter set a).

### M1 `[refactor]` + `[feature]`: contract scaffolding and hook points, inert (2.5–3.5 days)

- **Scope:** `annotation/{schema,vocab,provenance,config,samplesheet_columns}.py`, `assets/annotation/*` and generator; `table_keys.py`, `clustering/cellset.py`; config models (defaults legacy) and hooks H8 / H9; `workflows/lib/Annotation{Defaults,Settings,Preflight}.groovy`, `conf/annotation.config` and hooks H1–H4, H6, H7 in inert form; package data; `ci.yml` push trigger and `nf-metro validate` step.
- **Tests:** `test_vocab.py` (every WHB supercluster, SEA subclass and WMB class maps; NT covers every neuronal supercluster; `broad_class_for_map_first` never returns a sink); `test_schema.py`; `test_cellset.py` (identical to the legacy filter); `test_table_keys_sync.py` (reseg, original_seg and default paths, with and without suffix); `test_annotation_defaults.py` (`resolveMode` and `tableKeySuffix` in all four mode × flip combinations); `test_annotation_config_no_duplicates.py` (no param in both `annotation.config` and `nextflow.config` / `dwight.config`); `tests/test_config.py` (models, validators, species defaults, `hard_min_counts` coupling); **`test_legacy_script_pin.py`** (sha256 of the resolved legacy PREPARE / COMPUTE / FINALIZE script text; R-eng); **`test_rca_hooks.py`**; **`test_import_isolation.py`** (subprocess with ctm and spatialdata blocked imports `merxen.clustering_squidpy_stages`, `merxen.clustering.*`, `merxen.annotation.{schema,vocab,config}`).
- **Exit:** full `pytest` green; legacy clustering tests give equal `obs` / `uns` content; no metro change; the `main.nf` diff is limited to hook markers.

### M2 `[feature]`: reference store, `ANNOTATE_PANEL`, `ANNOTATE_REFERENCE_PREP` (4–5 days)

- **Scope:** `store.py`, `reference.py`, `panel.py` (same-panel intersection, set c, single-platform; declared-panel hash; `required_bundles.json`); CLIs `annotation-panel`, `annotation-reference-prep`, `annotation-store`; `annotation.nf` PANEL and PREP, wired only when a species is `map_first` or `--annotation_prepare_only`; SEA-AD download (pinned URL + sha256); metro stations; dwight paths and resources.
- **Tests** (`test_store.py`, `test_reference.py`, `test_panel.py`): hash stability and sensitivity (incl. `ANNOTATION_BUILDER_VERSION`); a crash leaves no partial bundle; concurrent `get_or_build` builds once (two processes); never deletes; `prune` dry-run lists only; copy, not symlink; `filter_lookup_to_tree` and auto-collapse; ctm runners monkeypatched (as in `test_mapmycells.py`); slow: a tiny real bundle (~200 cells, 3 levels); workflow: no `storeDir`; PREP once per unique (species, reference, panel); MAP waits for each pair × segmentation's required bundles (`groupKey` size), with same-panel (set c) and `per_platform` (five bundles) fixtures; no GPU lock.
- **Exit** (bundles on dwight in the SSD1 store): `whb_frontal_supc_clus` root markers match `research/pilot/whb_region/query_markers_panel_n30.json` (296 genes; allowing for H2AX after M0e); `seaad_mr_panel` and set-c bundles; `wmb_panel` for ag7 and VZG2 (10Xv3 build) with lookup parent coverage equal to `research/selfmap/panel_ref/query_markers_{ag7,vzg2}.json` and mapping-precompute sha256 equal to the Allen file's; `wmb_region_share` with windows for all MERFISH sections; `profiles.parquet` log-correlation ≥ 0.999 vs `exp/E1/01_stats_profile_validation.csv`; build times in the PR.

### M3 `[feature]`: MMC engine, standalone `merxen annotate`, shadow evaluation (3–4 days + ~1 day compute)

- **Scope:** `mapmycells_engine.py`, `pipeline.annotate_map`, `shadow.py`; `CLUSTERING_SQUIDPY_ANNOTATE_MAP` (process + metro; not called in legacy mode); provisional `ct_*` from raw thresholds.
- **Shadow programme** (standalone on published `*_clustered.h5ad`, table cells, `layers["counts"]`; outputs under `$A/shadow/`): (1) production baselines on the post-M0e set a for P7513, P1212, **P7113, P5011** on proseg_hybrid and **reseg** — soft / argmax / confident JSD with CIs, WHB–SEA agreement, coverage, implausible calls, COP, marker referee, below-60 SEA-rule cost; (2) **E8** segmentation comparison for P1212 and ag7 (confident cells per mm², foreign-marker fraction, platform soft JSD; feeds OD-B6, OD-B7); (3) **X1** platform rescaling (capped reference-pseudobulk factors) vs set a and set c on JSD, referee, held-out genes; (4) held-out-gene baseline (§5.8); (5) realised contamination and diffuse flag rates; (6) **LL value test** (does LL (vii) move referee outcomes > 2 points or coverage > 5 points? decides whether LL joins v1.1, OD-B8); with (1)'s below-60 cost it also scores the OD-B13 trigger (> 8 points of coverage lost on any dataset → the LL part of M10 moves before M8); (7) WHB vs SEA-AD glial JSD difference with CIs, for information (OD-B2 decided).
- **Deliverable:** `docs/acceptance/annotation-v1-preregistration.md` (§14 thresholds + measured baselines; baselines may only tighten; loosening needs your written approval in the gate PR).
- **Tests:** fake-mapper end-to-end on a tiny AnnData; the tidy parser on fixture extended JSON; query fingerprint; ctm assertion; thread exports and `BLAS=1`; no GPU lock.
- **Exit:** at bp ≥ 0.8, WHB supercluster calls agree ≥ 0.98 with the E1 (iii) pilot on shared cells (not at the noise floor: 99.85% seed-stable; R-fea); SEA-AD subclass agrees ≥ the seed floor (.945) − 0.02; wall time within §10.

### M3b `[feature]` (rev2): panel and platform generality (7.5–10 days + ~1–1.5 days compute)

- **Scope** (§8): `gene_ids.py` with the exact-case species test, the control registry with feature types from the Xenium source table and control probe / codeword names from the 10x and Vizgen documentation (OD-D12; the ProSeg transcript-filter `[bugfix]` goes to `main` from the same names before the first 5K or custom dataset is segmented), `panel_mode` `per_platform` with intersection runs, declared-panel hashes, `panel_family` (same species and platform) and subset bundles; `diagnostics.py` (trust states), `validated_panels.csv`; `resolvability.py` with the `whb_frontal_supc_clus_ho` and `wmb_selfmap_testset` builders (PREP writes resolvability, cell tables, summary, trust), thinning only cells with native counts ≥ D, D_max inheritance (replaced by pooled deep bins, user decision 2026-09-27, §8.3), and **the local isotonic threshold rule with split halves and Wilson bound, moved from M10 (+1–2 days)**; large-panel guards (large store with kept family reference markers, per-parent prefilter, PREP / MAP resources by panel size); **the 5K memory measurement** (§8.7); `annotation-panel-simulate`; report and provenance fields.
- **Tests:** registry = `remove_control_features` on current-panel fixtures; a synthetic Xenium 5K `var` (all control types) keeps only `Gene Expression`; MERSCOPE `Blank-12` removed; a real gene containing a token kept. Resolver: version suffixes; species mismatch → `refused`, incl. ag7 / VZG2 symbols under `species=human` and P7513 MERSCOPE symbols under `species=mouse` (`species_mismatch`); duplicates summed; symbol fallback; overrides; single-target aliases; unmapped listed; the ag7 symbols-as-IDs fixture → `refused`. Trust-state truth table; panel family (a different platform does not inherit); a zero-count probe or `min_cells` filtering does not change `panel_hash`; subset-bundle trigger. Resolvability on a synthetic 3-level reference with a planted non-resolvable level (not emitted); a planted near-threshold error band that the set-level rule would pass and the local rule rejects; split-half and Wilson checks; raise-only thresholds; floors = max(known, simulated); D_max inheritance and `resolvability_extrapolated` on a class that never reaches the top bins; `not_resolvable`. A synthetic 5K panel at ~1,000 counts (R-rob item 10): large store, kept reference markers, resources. `per_platform` wiring on two synthetic panels.
- **Exit:** **H18** (§14); `annotation-panel-simulate` reports for the 10x Xenium Prime 5K Human and Mouse lists and the Xenium Human / Mouse Brain panels (predicted levels, runtime, disk, peak RSS, prefilter comparison per level and class); **5K memory measurement**: WMB reference and query markers for the Xenium Prime 5K Mouse list, with and without the prefilter, at `--n_processors 8`, with wall time, peak RSS and disk, and PREP memory for > 1,000 genes set to that + 30% (OD-E8); resolvability runtime within §8.3 for ≤ 815 genes.

### M4 `[feature]`: `ANNOTATE_RESOLVE` v1 (3.5–5 days)

- **Scope:** `thresholds.py`, `consensus.py`, `composition.py`, `flags.py` (human), `pipeline.annotate_resolve`; full §4.1 schema, provenance and degraded modes; resolvability-gated emission with composition reweighting and local thresholds from the bundle tables; trust effects; `CLUSTERING_SQUIDPY_ANNOTATE_RESOLVE` (metro).
- **Tests (synthetic):** degraded-mode truth table (incl. refused SEA); COP suppression and COP → lineage; SEA confident disagreement; implausible fallback; floors per class × platform × panel and n < 50 inheritance; gate truth table: level (`full` / `broad_only` / `failed`) × warning flag, incl. the segmented-object warning and a provisional panel that warns without lowering `full`; reweighting changes emission for a planted composition shift; `not_resolvable`; soft vectors sum to 1; block-bootstrap reproducibility; beta-binomial flag detects a planted 30% spill with ≤ 2% on clean data; diffuse flag calibrated on a synthetic multinomial; uninformative switching; `validate_label_table`.
- **Exit:** shadow outputs for all 8 human datasets; gate levels and warnings as expected (§5.4); realised flag rates tabulated; coverage within ±0.05 of the M3 baseline (R-sci); resolvability changes no set-a label beyond classes with < 50 test cells or extrapolated bins (listed).

### M5 `[feature]`: pipeline wiring and map-first hierarchy (5–6 days)

- **Scope:** `clustering/{representation,map_first,stability}.py`; `CLUSTERING_SQUIDPY_COMPUTE_CPU`; `CLUSTERING_MAP_FIRST` and hook H5; species-resolved mode and suffixed keys (H2); `runMapMyCells` (H3); MENDER `unassigned_state_policy`; preflight gating (H4) and failure summary (H6); dwight `maxForks`; metro.
- **Tests:** `test_map_first.py` (`hierarchical_cluster` categorical, non-null, unique; `neuron_split_label`; broad-only datasets and `broad_only` panels give `unresolved` leaves; H5AD ids = parquet `in_table` ids); h5ad and TableModel-zarr round trip of every new `obs` / `uns` field (R-eng); `test_mender_smoke.py` under both policies; cortical-depth fixtures with `Mixed/Unknown` / `Oligodendrocyte lineage`; workflow: COMPUTE_CPU without GPU lock, legacy COMPUTE pinned, `runMapMyCells` false and terminal stage never `mapmycells` in map_first (`test_mender_module.py`), failed pair listed, preflight gating, per-row `mouse_section_regions` / `anatomical_region` in `samples_json`.
- **Exit:** end-to-end map_first on P7513 and P1212 proseg_hybrid (after the M-1 snapshot) writes `_mapfirst` tables; MENDER and cortical depth complete; viewer loads reseg (manual); legacy tables untouched (sha256 of the zarr table group).

### M6 `[feature]`: mouse multi-region (3–4 days; after M3b and M4)

- **Scope:** `mouse_regions.py` and the pruned re-map; `mouse_gate.py`; mouse flags (spill-over with derived genes and FPR check, astro low-count, F1); samplesheet column; the `avg_correlation` shadow study (§7.3), pre-registered.
- **Tests:** region inference on synthetic tiles (1% rule; < 200 tiles skips); two-tier rule, never-drop and small-subclass inheritance; override and `none`; lookup filter + `nodes_to_drop` without zero-marker error; gate truth table; the derived microglia set on ag7 equals E3's; spill-over on planted counts; samplesheet parsing; slow: an ag7 30k subset.
- **Exit:** E7 reproduced on ag7 and VZG2 original_seg (regions = manual sets; class changes outside dropped nodes ≤ 0.25%); invalid VZG2 proseg_hybrid `failed`; VZG2 original_seg and ag7 not `failed`.

### M6b `[feature]` (rev2): mouse AP-axis validation and rule hardening (4–5 days + ~1.5 days compute; after M6)

- **Scope** (§7.8): `scripts/acceptance/mouse_ap_axis.py` (direct harm metric; oracle harm as diagnostic); the MERFISH-638850 expression download (~7.6 GB, public Allen data, to `/srv/storage`; approved 2026-09-26, OD-C8), the three WMB panel bundles and mapping of the 38 in-scope sections on those gene sets; leave-neighbours-out region shares and subclass homes; structure-level AON homes; variants (a)–(d), (f) behind `MouseRegionConfig` ((e) withdrawn); `estimate_ap()` and all-section G4 windows.
- **Tests:** coupling, never-drop and tile-size variants on synthetic tiles; leave-neighbours-out excludes the section and its neighbours; the AP estimate on held-out MERFISH sections (within one section step); per-bin summary on a planted-sliver set.
- **Exit:** MO10 passes under the pre-registered selection rule; the chosen variant written into the preregistration; MO2, MO3 still pass on ag7 and VZG2. MO11 is scored when the first real anterior section arrives.

### M7 `[feature]`: `ANNOTATION_REPORT` (4–6 days)

- **Scope:** `report.py`, `ANNOTATION_REPORT` (metro), `acceptance_metrics.json`, incl. the panel card and AP items (§9).
- **Tests:** soft JSD with CIs, aligned-bin concordance, depth ordering and replication metric on synthetic inputs; builds with and without cortical depth and mask, and for provisional / refused panels.
- **Exit:** human reports for P7513 and P1212 reviewed by you; the mouse items (§9 item 10) built and tested on synthetic inputs. The ag7 and VZG2 original_seg reports need M6 / M6b and are accepted in M9.

### M8 `[feature]`: human acceptance, default flip, gate H (3–5 days + compute)

- **Runs:** P7513, P1212, P7113, P5011, all 4 segmentations through the pipeline, plus `run_acceptance.py`, `heldout_genes.py`, `marker_referee.py`, `compare_legacy.py` and H18 → `results/acceptance/<date>/summary.json` + HTML.
- **If the §14 human criteria pass:** flip PR into the integration branch (`[feature]`): `clustering_squidpy_mode_human = "map_first"`; human `annotation_mode_mapmycells_stage = "skip"`; human table-key suffix `""` (the snapshot keeps legacy); deprecation warnings; docs; change notes (new `broad_class` values `Mixed/Unknown` and `Oligodendrocyte lineage`, `hierarchical_cluster` format, MENDER semantics, trust states, `not_resolvable`).
- **Then gate H:** merge `main` in; open integration → `main` (CI and dwight slow tests green, acceptance linked, `bump-my-version` next minor — 0.2.0 if H is the first gate — and tag). If OD-B13 fired at M3, the LL part of M10 is in this acceptance.

### M9 `[feature]`: mouse acceptance, default flip, gate M (2.5–4 days + compute)

- **Runs:** ag7 (all segmentations); VZG2 original_seg; VZG2 reseg / proseg_* after M0a (MO9); the invalid VZG2 proseg_hybrid as negative control; the M6b MERFISH table (MO10); review of the ag7 and VZG2 original_seg annotation reports (M7's mouse items).
- **If the §14 mouse criteria pass:** flip PR for `clustering_squidpy_mode_mouse`, then gate M (next minor). Until MO11 passes, sections estimated at AP < 6.0 mm carry the banner "anterior region rule not yet validated on a real section".

### M10 `[feature]` (v1.1): LL, calibrated probabilities, measured recipe, gate v2 (4–6 days)

- **Scope:** `likelihood.py` (variant vii; factors capped ±2 log2; moved before M8 if OD-B13 fires); `calibration.py` on `resolvability.py` (calibrated-probability output from the v1 isotonic tables and reweighting; measured spill and platform factors per platform once data exist); floors with binomial CIs; SEA thresholds from its table; gate v2 (bundle-predicted coverage + self-thinning + D); the LL vote in the degraded-mode table. Re-measure D, the below-60 cost and tier coverage with (vii).
- **Exit:** re-acceptance of H1–H3, H6, H7, H9 with v1.1 on the 4 pairs; flipping `thresholds.mode = "simulation_calibrated"` is a separate PR after re-acceptance (OD-D11); gate 1.1.

### M11 `[feature]` (v1.1): opt-in within-supercluster de novo split (5 days)

- **Scope:** §6.4 with `adaptive_split.rule = "count_split_merge"` and `leaf_source="denovo"` opt-in (OD-B1).
- **Tests:** single population at 20 and 70 counts → K = 1; planted 3-type mixture → 3; depth-only split → 1; size folding; regime diagnostic; projection validation.
- **Exit:** §6.4 validation (held-out donors, reseg, non-trivial passes reported separately); stays default-off unless you decide otherwise.

### M12 `[refactor]`: legacy removal (one release after both flips; gate 12)

Delete the deprecated labeller, its params and `ClusteringSquidpyAnnotationConfig`; delete MAPMYCELLS (OD-D1, adopted); retire the GPU COMPUTE env if unused (OD-D2); update the workflow tests pinning these strings.

### M13 `[feature]` (rev2; rescoped in rev3): new-panel onboarding and simulation-based validation (7–10 developer-days + CPU compute, §10; simulation after M3b and M4; real-data QC wiring after M6, M6b (mouse G4 windows / AP estimate) and M7; parallel with M5–M9)

- **Scope** (OD-E1 / OD-E9, decided 2026-09-26: simulation only; no downloads, no public data, no re-segmentation): `scripts/acceptance/new_panel.py` and `annotation-panel-simulate --gate-p` — the gate-P programme per family (§8.8, §14): leave-one-donor-out HO bundles for the two other frontal WHB donors (`build_whb_frontal_ho(donor)`), a second disjoint WMB test draw from cells the marker build did not use (topped up per class), seeds 0 / 1, frozen-threshold evaluation on pooled tested sets (≥ 200 confident calls, deep bins pooled), the CI-aware replicate spread, the stress recipes, reweighting (reference composition, class-balanced, and a family dataset's depth histogram when one exists; Kish effective n), the class set C_P and per-(level, class) `validated_min_depth`, error-structure and prefilter checks, the NP1–NP9 report, and the dry run on set a, ag7 and VZG2 (2.5–3 days); the `validated_panels.csv` column `validation_basis`, `validated_panel_levels.csv` and the per-(level, class) trust effects of `simulation` rows in RESOLVE (`ct_<L>_validated`, margins kept, emission unchanged by promotion, the 10% warning rule; 0.5–1 day); `annotation/real_qc.py` — the downgrade-only production QC of §8.8 wired into RESOLVE and the report (human marker-consistency referee, paired intersection-run concordance, flag-rate summary, gene-complexity gap, 5K prefilter spot check; mouse reuses G1–G5, with the G4 all-section windows and AP estimate from M6b; warnings only on the seeded `real_data` families until their species gate; 1–1.5 days, reusing `marker_referee.py` from M3 and G2 from M6); first families: the 10x Xenium Prime 5K Human and Mouse lists, the Xenium Human Brain (266) and Mouse Brain (248) panels, and each in-house custom panel as its gene list arrives (runs and PR evidence, 1 day); tests and integration (0.5–1 day) [L]. The component estimates (5.5–7.5 days) are before the ~30% contingency included in the 7–10 days.
- **Tests:** frozen thresholds applied to a replicate with a planted donor shift fail NP4, while replicates differing only by binomial noise pass it; tested sets never hold < 200 confident calls and count each test cell once; a stress recipe that removes a class's markers fails NP6; a planted sink absorbing > 5% of a class fails NP7; one failing class leaves the other classes of its level validated; a property test that promotion (`provisional` → `simulation`) never changes an emitted label or status, only `ct_<L>_validated`, the banner and the warning flag; a property test over all QC outcomes: real-data QC can lower a dataset's gate level or set a warning, and never raises a trust state or removes a margin; `simulation` rows keep the margins; `validation_basis` round trip.
- **Exit:** the dry run first: set a, ag7 and VZG2 pass gate P at broad (human) / class (mouse) for every C_P class and at supercluster / subclass for the classes H18 expects; otherwise the criteria are unattainable and are revised in a PR you approve before any new family is scored. Then an NP report per onboarded family (C_P and the excluded classes, validated levels per class and depth, `validated_min_depth` and `tested_max_depth`, replicate spread, stress drops, runtime, peak RSS); each family passing gate P is promoted by its own gate-P PR (a `validated_panels.csv` row with `validation_basis = simulation`, its `validated_panel_levels.csv` rows and the simulation evidence; your approval); failing families stay `provisional` or `broad_only` with the reason documented; the real-data QC outcomes on the current in-house datasets (P7513, P1212, P7113, P5011, ag7, VZG2 original_seg) are tabulated as a regression, and any warning on a real-data-validated dataset (warnings only until its species gate, §8.8) is explained in the PR.

## 13. Testing plan

Per-milestone test lists are in §12; this section adds the shared fixtures and properties.

### 13.1 Unit tests (fast, synthetic; paths mirror the package)

- **Fixtures** (`tests/test_annotation/conftest.py`, `tests/test_clustering/conftest.py`): synthetic references (5–8 types × 60 genes, NB counts, 2-level tree; a 3-level variant with a planted non-resolvable level); queries at 15 / 30 / 60 / 500 / 1,000 counts with planted gene factors and spill-over; synthetic `var` tables (MERSCOPE symbols + `Blank-N`; Xenium `gene_ids` + `feature_types`; a 5,000-gene Xenium 5K-like table with all control types; the ag7 symbols-as-IDs failure; mouse symbols under `species=human` and vice versa); the Xenium Prime 5K Mouse gene list as a panel stub; MERFISH-like tiles with planted slivers; a fake extended-JSON writer; a tiny SpatialData (patterns from `tests/test_analysis/test_clustering_squidpy.py`).
- **Modules:** `tests/test_annotation/test_{schema,vocab,config,provenance,store,reference,panel,gene_ids,diagnostics,resolvability,mapmycells_engine,shadow,thresholds,consensus,composition,flags,mouse_regions,mouse_gate,pipeline,report}.py` (+ `likelihood`, `calibration` in v1.1); `tests/test_clustering/test_{cellset,import_isolation,representation,map_first,stability}.py` (+ `count_split`, `adaptive_split`, `projection` in v1.1); `tests/test_qc/test_registration.py`; `tests/test_io/test_vzg2.py`; `tests/test_config.py`.
- **Properties:** degraded-mode, consensus and trust-state truth tables; floor inheritance and the unknown-panel max rule; resolvability emission (local isotonic rule, split halves, Wilson bound, D_max inheritance, composition reweighting), raise-only thresholds, `not_resolvable`; declared-panel hash stability; the exact-case species test; control registry = `remove_control_features` on current panels; resolver order and refusals; gate level × warning flag (a provisional panel warns but never lowers the level); soft vectors sum to 1; flag null ≤ 2% on clean data and planted-spill detection; derived flag genes; `hierarchical_cluster` non-null, categorical, unique; schema (`cell_id` uniqueness, index equality); cell-set identity with legacy; the store never deletes, builds once under concurrency, routes large panels; no zero-marker error; import isolation; h5ad / zarr round trip.

### 13.2 Workflow string tests

- **New:** `test_annotation_module.py` (names; no `storeDir`; PREP resources incl. large panels; required-bundle collection per pair × segmentation (`groupKey`), incl. `per_platform` and set c; no GPU lock on the six new processes; thread exports, `BLAS=1`, `PYTHONPATH`; publish paths); `test_map_first_subworkflow.py`; `test_legacy_script_pin.py`; `test_rca_hooks.py`; `test_env_lock_sync.py`; `test_table_keys_sync.py`; `test_annotation_defaults.py`; `test_annotation_config_no_duplicates.py`.
- **Updated:** `test_clustering_squidpy_module.py` (new processes; species switch; legacy heredoc unchanged; the `mapmycells.nf` species string while legacy exists); `test_analysis_segmentation_branches.py:69-70` (`"annotation.nf": "/${segmentation}/annotation_report"`); `test_mender_module.py:52-72` (order unchanged; `runMapMyCells` false in map_first; terminal never `mapmycells` in skip mode); `test_metro_map.py`; `test_dwight_profile.py` (GPU lock only on GPU processes; `maxForks`; both stores); `tests/test_io/test_samplesheet.py`.
- **Consumers:** `test_clustering_squidpy.py` (legacy content-equal; warnings only after the flip); `test_mapmycells.py` (M0b incl. `:698-700`); `test_cortical_depth.py`; `test_mender.py`; `test_mender_smoke.py`.

### 13.3 Slow integration and 13.4 acceptance runs

- **Slow** (`@pytest.mark.slow`; skip unless `MERXEN_TEST_DATA_DIR` and `MERXEN_TEST_REFERENCE_STORE` are set): 2k-cell subsets of P7513 M / X, P1212 M, ag7, VZG2 original_seg, one MERFISH-638850 section (from M6b) and, from M13, a 2k-cell 5K-panel query simulated from WMB test cells (no public 5K data, OD-E1) (`scripts/acceptance/make_test_subsets.py`; never committed); bundle load, gene IDs, real MMC, resolve, map_first end to end, report, schema; < 15 min on 6 processes.

## 14. Acceptance criteria (pre-registered; must pass before each gate)

**Rules.** Thresholds are fixed **now** and copied into `docs/acceptance/annotation-v1-preregistration.md` at M3 (the MO10 rule variant at M6b). Baselines may only **tighten** a threshold; loosening needs your written approval in the gate PR (R-sci). **Held out:** P7113 and P5011 (donors), reseg (segmentation, P7513 / P1212), the MERFISH AP series (MO10; region shares recomputed leaving each section and its neighbours out) and, for gate P, the simulation replicates on which frozen thresholds are tested (other held-out donors or test draws and seeds, NP4); none is used for tuning beyond the pre-registered selection rule, and their results are reported separately. Metrics come from `acceptance_metrics.json` and the acceptance scripts, on proseg_hybrid unless stated; [L] = first-run target.

### Human (gate H, at M8)

| ID | Criterion | Threshold | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|
| H1 | Soft broad JSD (7 classes, all table cells, whole section; 95% block-bootstrap CI and shared-mask value reported) | P7513 ≤ 0.17; P1212 ≤ 0.17; **P7113 ≤ 0.17; P5011 ≤ 0.31** (held out) | Soft .139 / .129 / .136 / .279 + 0.03 (`review_scientific/confident_jsd_bias_extra.txt`, E2 `cells_native`); E1 `real_jsd.csv` (iii) .132 / .143 under its scheme, so P1212 gets the larger margin [M]; legacy .51 / 1.00 / .66 / .44 |
| H2 | `flag_implausible` share of table cells | ≤ 1% per dataset | E1 (iii) 0.3–0.6% |
| H3 | WHB–SEA 7-class agreement, table cells ≥ 20 counts | P7513 M / X ≥ 0.90; P1212 M / X ≥ 0.80; P7113, P5011 ≥ 0.80 | E1 .932 / .917 / .826 / .904 |
| H4 | Held-out-gene enrichment (§5.8), per platform | Fold ≥ 3 and AUROC ≥ 0.70 in ≥ 6 of 7 broad classes on P7513, P1212, P7113 [L] | Non-circular (R-sci) |
| H5 | COP control | Confident COP supercluster ≤ 2%; confident broad OPC ≤ 10%; COP-derived OPC share reported | E1; `review_scientific/cop_demotion_rule.csv` |
| H6 | Simulation precision of the v1 raw thresholds (archived E2 HO contaminated simulation, reweighted to each dataset's composition) | Broad ≥ 0.90 at D ≥ 15; supercluster ≥ 0.85 at D ≥ 30, classes with n ≥ 50 [M] | Replaces the draft's circular curve test; H18 recomputes it in production |
| H7 | Confident broad coverage of table cells | P7513 M ≥ .62, X ≥ .44; P7113 M ≥ .67, X ≥ .39; P1212 M ≥ .34, X ≥ .29; P5011 M ≥ .30, X ≥ .40 | E2 LL-rule coverage − 0.10 (SEA-rule cost unmeasured until M3) |
| H8 | Gate levels | P1212_M and P5011_M `broad_only` (P5011_M with the warning flag); none `failed`; the rest `full` (mechanical, given A). Real-data QC (§8.8) only warns on these seeded datasets before this gate; its expected warnings are listed in the gate PR (e.g. P5011's paired broad JSD, soft .279 > 0.20; the marker-referee thresholds were measured only on P7513 and P1212, E1) | §5.4, §8.8 |
| H9 | Canonical-marker plausibility of confident `ct_broad` (marker pseudo-labels, `data/P7513` §C, `data/P1212` §4) | P7513 M ≥ 0.90, X ≥ 0.83; P1212 M / X ≥ 0.75 [M] | Legacy 69.6% / 44.2%; MMC at bp ≥ 0.9 93% / 85% |
| H10 | Marker referee on new-vs-legacy broad disputes | Markers side with the new label in ≥ 70% | E1 referee method |
| H12 | Cortical depth from manual pia/WM boundaries (label-independent) | P7513, P7113: Upper-layer IT < Deep-layer IT < Deep-layer NP/CT/6b medians with non-overlapping 95% CIs on both platforms. **All 4 pairs incl. P1212, P5011, at broad level:** oligodendrocytes WM > GM on both platforms [L] | R-sci; outputs exist for all 4 pairs × 2 platforms [H] |
| H13 | Contracts | MENDER completes on all 4 pairs under both unassigned policies; cortical-depth violins; H5AD and zarr id sets identical; legacy tables untouched before the flip (sha256); viewer on reseg (manual) | Wiring map §c |
| H14 | Resources | Annotation wall ≤ 2.5 h per pair (all segmentations); MAP peak RSS ≤ 16 GB; PREP ≤ 2.5 h per validated panel incl. resolvability; no GPU | §10 |
| H15 | Reproducibility | Identical re-run → identical DataFrame content; seed 0 vs 1 changes ≤ 1% of confident broad labels | E5: 99.85% stable at bp ≥ 0.8 |
| H16 | Flag transparency | Realised rates for every flag × class × platform; strata > 15% marked uninformative (mechanical) | §5.6 |
| H17 | Segmentation hold-out (reseg, P7513 / P1212) | H1 thresholds + 0.02; H2; H9 | R-sci |
| H18 | (rev2) Resolvability regression on the validated panels, from production PREP | Human set a (post-M0e): broad and supercluster emitted for every class with ≥ 50 test cells at depths from its v1 floor plus one grid step up to D_max; WHB cluster not emitted; simulated precision at 0.73 / 0.69 ≥ 0.90 / 0.85 at D ≥ 15 / 30 (local rule). ag7, VZG2: class and subclass emitted at min(dataset median depth, D_max(c)) for every class with ≥ 50 test cells. All three `validated`. No validated threshold would be raised, or each exception is listed in the gate PR | §8.3; E2; mouse report §4.2 |

**Human flip rule:** H1–H3, H5, H7–H10, H12–H16 on P7513 and P1212; H1–H3, H7, H8, H12 on P7113 and P5011; H4 on P7513, P1212, P7113; H6 and H18 once; H17 on reseg. Any failure needs a fix or a written, user-approved exception in the gate PR. *Decided 2026-09-27 (OD-B14):* H2 and H4, and the H7 / H18 exceptions, are decided at M8 after re-measuring with M4's final rules; the M8 gate PR brings a fix or a written exception proposal for each. The draft's split-rule criterion (H11) belongs to M11.

### Mouse (gate M, at M9)

| ID | Criterion | Threshold | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|
| MO1 | Class marker consistency over **all marker-pseudo-confident cells** (fixed denominator; confident-cell value reported) | ag7 proseg_hybrid ≥ 0.89; VZG2 original_seg ≥ 0.84 | .911 (`review_feasibility/mo1_ag7_marker_consistency.csv`), .856 (`review_scientific/mouse_vzg2_marker_consistency_panelLK.csv`) |
| MO2 | Pruning mechanics (not validation) | T2 after pruning ≤ 0.1%; class changes outside dropped nodes ≤ 0.25%; subclass ≤ 1.5% | E7 |
| MO3 | Inferred regions | = manual sets for ag7 and VZG2 | E7 §4 |
| MO4 | Soft class composition vs AP-matched MERFISH | VZG2 original_seg Immune ±0.5 and Astro-Epen ±3 points (MERFISH .31–.33: 1.50–1.56 / 17.2–17.5); ag7 ±1 / ±5 [L; age caveat] | Replaces the draft's circular MO4 / MO6 (R-sci) |
| MO5 | Spill-over flag | VZG2 held-out microglial-gene enrichment ≥ 10×; astrocyte FPR ≤ 0.5%; never shown as a count | E3 (35–40×) |
| MO6 | F1 rate after pruning | 0.2–1.0% | E7 |
| MO7 | Mouse gate | Invalid VZG2 proseg_hybrid `failed`; VZG2 original_seg and ag7 not `failed` | §7.6 |
| MO8 | Resources | ≤ 1.25 h per 4-segmentation section; PREP ≤ 2 h per panel incl. resolvability; MAP peak ≤ 12 GB; PREP peak ≤ 40 GB at 8 processes (query markers measured 20–27 GB at 16) | §10, §8.7 |
| MO9 | VZG2 after the M0a re-run | Registration passes; reseg / proseg_hybrid soft class JSD vs original_seg ≤ 0.05 [L] | Gates the *use* of those outputs, not the flip |
| MO10 | (rev2) AP-axis harm on MERFISH-638850 with the production rule (M6b), per AP bin over the 38 in-scope sections, for each of 3 gene sets, with region shares computed leaving each section and its neighbours out. Harm = share of confident Allen-labelled cells (`average_correlation_score ≥ 0.6` and our class bp ≥ 0.9) whose class or subclass is in the production drop list | Per bin mean harm ≤ 0.10% and per-section maximum ≤ 0.30%; T2 after pruning ≤ 0.1% in every bin; missed regions reported and allowed only where that section's harm ≤ 0.1%; oracle-relative harm reported as a diagnostic [L] | §7.8 (direct Allen-label proxy today: AP 3.0–4.5 mean / max 0.15% / 0.70%, every other bin ≤ 0.08% / 0.11%) |
| MO11 | (rev2) First real anterior coronal section (AP < 6.0 mm) | Inferred regions = manual anatomy, or differ only by slivers < 1% of tiles with harm ≤ 0.1%; MO2 mechanics; gate not `failed`; G4 within warning bands or explained [L] | §7.8 step 3 |

**Mouse flip rule:** MO1–MO8 and MO10. MO11 is required before map_first results from anterior sections (AP < 6.0 mm) lose their banner; it does not block the flip.

### New panel family (gate P; before a family is listed as `validated`)

**Simulation only** (OD-E1 / OD-E9, decided 2026-09-26). Scored by `scripts/acceptance/new_panel.py` (`annotation-panel-simulate --gate-p`) on the family's declared panel: held-out Allen reference cells restricted to the panel, thinned to the grid (and weighted to a family dataset's depth histogram when one exists, label-free), perturbed and mapped with the production configuration (§8.3, §8.8). No real dataset is needed or used for promotion. Notation: target_L = 0.90 (broad, lineage, NT, class) or 0.85 (supercluster, subclass); **target⁺_L** = target_L + 0.05 at ≥ 60 counts and + 0.10 below 60 counts, capped at 0.97 (the provisional targets, §8.2). These margins are stricter than for the real-data-validated panels (H6: point precision ≥ target_L; the §8.3 emission rule: Wilson bound ≥ target − 0.02 and coverage ≥ 0.2), because simulation is optimistic (E2 verdict 5: native low-count cells carry 30–45% more genes than thinned cells; up to 5–10 points below ~60 counts on Xenium). Replicates, stress recipes and the evaluation constants: §3.7 (`gate_p_*`).

**Evaluation rules** (rev3 check; pre-registered with the thresholds). They keep the criteria passable at the sample sizes the references supply. A Wilson bound ≥ 0.95 needs ≥ 73 confident calls even at 100% precision, and the three frontal WHB donors together hold few glia on set a: Microglia 1,198 cells (483 with ≥ 60 panel counts, 28 with ≥ 120), OPC 1,833, Vascular 37, Fibroblast 25; the default HO donor alone has 243 Microglia (R45) [H].
- **Pooled held-out calls.** NP3, NP6 and NP7 are scored at the frozen thresholds on all held-out calls: human, the default donor's check half (split halves, §8.3) plus both other donors, each mapped against the bundle built without it; mouse, both draws. NP4 then compares the replicates.
- **Tested sets.** Per (level L, class c), a depth bin is tested on its own only if it holds ≥ n_min = 200 confident calls (`gate_p_min_confident_n`). From the deep end, bins are pooled cumulatively into a "≥ d" set, each test cell counted once at its deepest bin, until the set holds ≥ n_min calls. The set's shallowest bin is **D_P(L, c)**; its result applies to every depth ≥ D_P, and bins deeper than D_P are marked extrapolated (as §8.3 does beyond D_max). A (level, class) with < n_min confident calls in total is `not_evaluable`. Wilson bounds on reweighted sets use the Kish effective n. *User decision 2026-09-27 (H18 follow-up): one rule for §8.3 resolvability and gate P* — the §8.3 emission uses this pooling with n_min = 50 (`min_confident_n`) and judges each tested set by its point precision and its Wilson bound (Kish n); a cell's deepest bin is taken over all its calls before any class or confidence filter, and every bin at or above D_P that holds n_min calls itself is also tested on its own (both tests cover it; M3b review 2).
- **Class set C_P.** The classes with ≥ 700 pooled test cells (`gate_p_class_min_test_cells`, ≈ n_min / 0.30), fixed from the test-cell table before any cell is mapped. The second mouse draw tops every class up to 700 where the 10Xv3 cells unused by the marker build allow; it takes ≤ 5% of any cluster's cells, which bounds leakage into the Allen means [L]. C_P must hold ≥ 90% of the pooled test cells, and the PR lists the excluded classes with their share of the reference composition.
- **Per-class records.** A (level, class) is validated when NP3–NP7 pass for it. `validated_min_depth(L, c)` is the shallowest bin from which every bin below D_P is tested on its own and passes NP3, and the ≥ D_P set passes too. The record (§4.7) carries it, with D_P as `tested_max_depth`, or else the failing criterion. A failing or unevaluable class stays provisional per class (§8.2) and does not block the others.
- **Expected depth** (NP5): the median native panel counts of a family dataset when one exists, else the vendor's or the panel design's median depth, stated in the PR.
- **Dry run.** Before any new family is scored, the programme runs on the seeded families (set a, ag7, VZG2). They must pass at broad (human) / class (mouse) for every C_P class, and at supercluster / subclass for the classes H18 expects. If they do not, the criterion (statistic, n_min or margin) is unattainable rather than the panel bad; it is revised in a PR you approve (loosening rule, §14 Rules), and the revision is recorded before any new family is scored.

| ID | Criterion | Threshold | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|
| NP1 | Gene IDs and controls (declared panel file) | ≥ 95% of non-control features resolved to the run's species (≥ 98% when the vendor supplies Ensembl IDs); the exact-case species test passes; every control probe / codeword type named in the 10x or Vizgen documentation is removed by the registry and none reaches the mapping query; the unresolved list reviewed | §8.4 |
| NP2 | Panel coverage | Root markers ≥ 10; every root child with n ≥ 50 has ≥ 10 markers; weak and collapsed parents accepted in the PR | §8.2 |
| NP3 | Precision and coverage at the panel's depth (base recipe R1_contam_HO, seed 0, pooled held-out calls at the frozen thresholds) | For each level up to `max_leaf_level` and each class with ≥ n_min confident calls, at every tested set from `validated_min_depth(L, c)` up to and including the ≥ D_P set: point precision ≥ target⁺_L (the margin of the set's shallowest bin), Wilson 95% lower bound ≥ target_L and coverage ≥ 0.30, reweighted both to the reference's natural composition within the class and to a class-balanced one; values on a family dataset's depth histogram and composition reported when one exists [L] | Stricter than H6 and the §8.3 emission rule. At n = 200 the bound (0.910 at 0.95, 0.851 at 0.90) binds only when reweighting shrinks the effective n |
| NP4 | Stability across held-out donors and seeds (frozen thresholds) | Replicates: human, leave-one-donor-out over H19.30.002, H19.30.001 and H18.30.002 (each with its own HO bundle) × seeds 0 / 1; mouse, the two disjoint test draws × seeds 0 / 1 (the Allen means contain every donor, so a mouse donor cannot be held out; the per-donor spread is reported from the ABC `donor_label` [L]). Per (level, class), at every tested set: each replicate with ≥ 100 confident calls there (`gate_p_replicate_min_confident_n`) has point precision ≥ target_L. Where every replicate has ≥ 100 calls, the range of the seed-averaged donor (or draw) precisions is ≤ max(0.03, 3.5 × pooled SE), with pooled SE = √(p̄(1 − p̄)/n̄); by sampling alone, three equally precise replicates exceed 2 × SE 33% of the time, but 3.5 × SE only 3.5% (two replicates: 1.3%). Seed 0 vs 1 changes ≤ 2% of confident labels per validated level [L] | Seed noise 5.5% of SEA-AD subclass (E5), 1.6–3.0% of WMB subclass labels (E7); bp ≥ 0.8 cells 99.85% stable |
| NP5 | Resolvability consistency | Per (level, class), the §8.3 emission decisions re-derived in each replicate agree with the base run at every depth bin with ≥ 50 test cells, except at most the bin adjacent to the emission boundary; each t* varies ≤ 0.05 across replicates at tested sets; a class is not validated at L if its cells at the family's expected depth would be > 50% `resolvability_extrapolated` [L] | §8.3 (D_max, extrapolation) |
| NP6 | Sensitivity to contamination and gene-efficiency perturbations | With the frozen thresholds, pooled over every held-out donor or draw at seed 0: spill 0.35 (base 0.25); gene efficiency LogNormal(0, 1.0) (base 0.8); per-gene log2 factors drawn from the measured human cross-platform offsets (capped ±2). At NP3's tested sets (those left with < n_min stressed calls are pooled with the next deeper set): point precision ≥ target_L and Wilson lower bound ≥ target_L − 0.02 (the real-data emission rule, no margin), and a drop in point precision vs the base recipe not significantly above 0.05 (one-sided 95%); the clean (thinning-only) upper bound and coverage changes reported [L] | E2 R1_contam_HO; per-pair offsets −0.12 to +1.52 log2 (D-A7) |
| NP7 | Error structure (in-silico analogue of H2 / H5) | Human: confident calls to sink or region-implausible nodes ≤ 1% of confident calls. Human and mouse: at every tested set of a validated (level, class), no single wrong node receives > 5% of the class's confident calls (e.g. COP-like over-calling) [L] | H2, H5, D-A6 |
| NP8 | Cross-panel support (only when the family will be paired with a different panel) | The intersection panel with each intended partner meets NP3 at broad level; otherwise cross-platform statistics for such pairs stay broad-level and flagged (§8.5). Real paired concordance is production QC (downgrade-only, §8.8) [L] | §8.5 |
| NP9 | Resources, reproducibility | PREP incl. resolvability and the gate-P replicates within 1.5× of §8.7 / §10 (for 5K, of the M3b measurement); peak RSS of every PREP step, query markers included, within the reserve; above 1,000 genes the per-parent prefilter agrees with the unfiltered lookup ≥ 0.95 per validated level and class with n ≥ 50 at bp ≥ 0.8, with no parent below 5 markers; identical re-run → identical bundle and table content | H14 / H15; §8.7 |

**Gate-P rule:** NP1, NP2 and NP9 for the family (NP8 when it will be paired with a different panel) and NP3–NP7 per (level, class), all from simulation and scored under the evaluation rules above. `validated_max_level` is the deepest level ≤ `max_leaf_level` at which every class of C_P is validated. It is the family's headline level, and promotion requires at least broad (human) / class (mouse). The `validated_panels.csv` row carries `validation_basis = simulation`; the `validated_panel_levels.csv` rows carry each (level, class)'s status, `validated_min_depth` and `tested_max_depth` (§4.7). The family keeps the provisional emission rule and margins in production, so promotion never changes what is emitted (§8.2). Promotion by a PR you approve (into the integration branch before the first species gate, to `main` after, §2.2). Real datasets never promote: the production QC (§8.8) can only warn, force `broad_only` or `not_resolvable` for a dataset, or trigger a demotion PR.

## 15. Documentation updates

- **`docs/stages/annotation.md`** (new): purpose; references (versions, sha256, licences); panel-aware markers; mapping configuration; **panels (gene IDs, controls, diagnostics, resolvability, trust states)**; thresholds, floors, gates (human, mouse), degraded modes; flags ("report-only"); soft composition; outputs; parameters; compute incl. 5K; what v1 does not do (§5.9); **known limitations**, including the 4 WMB subclasses with no 10Xv3 reference cells (`157 RN Spp1 Glut`, `279 PSV Pax2 Gly-Gaba`, `280 NLL-po Pax7 Gaba`, `297 CU-ECU Pax2 Gly-Gaba`; Multiome-only, ≤ 0.2% of any in-scope posterior section), which cannot be assigned (§7.8, R38); and that new panels are validated by simulation only, with what the simulation does not capture and the downgrade-only real-data QC (OD-E1, §8.8, R42–R44).
- **`docs/panels.md`** (new): onboarding a Xenium 5K, custom Xenium or custom MERSCOPE panel — `annotation-panel-simulate` (and `--gate-p`), the simulation-only validation policy and its limits (OD-E1, §8.8), the simulation criteria NP1–NP9, the gate-P PR, the `validated_panels.csv` and `validated_panel_levels.csv` formats (`validation_basis`; per (level, class) status, `validated_min_depth`, `tested_max_depth`), first run, the panel card, the downgrade-only real-data QC and how a demotion PR works.
- **Stage docs:** `clustering-squidpy.md` (species-resolved modes; map-first branches and leaves; `unresolved`, `not_resolvable_panel`; QC Leiden; v1.1 split; legacy deprecated with its failures); `mapmycells.md` (legacy / diagnostic status; `annotation_mode_mapmycells_stage`; M0b cache change); `mender.md` (`hierarchical_cluster`, `ct_mender_state`, `unassigned_state_policy`, provisional panels in cohort outputs); `cortical-depth.md` (`broad_class` vocabulary incl. `Mixed/Unknown`, `Oligodendrocyte lineage`; the suffix); `qc.md` (registration check for MERSCOPE and Xenium, `qc_registration_strict`); `spatialdata-build.md` (VZG2 transform-override note; control feature types per platform).
- **`docs/outputs.md`** (label parquet schema, statuses, flags, new `obs` columns, `annotation_out`, `annotation_report`, `panel_report.json`, both store layouts, suffixed keys); **`configuration.md`** (all new params with species defaults; deprecated params); **`samplesheet.md`** (`mouse_section_regions` with the §7.8 AP scope; `anatomical_region`); **`pipeline.md`, `metro-map.md`** (regenerated SVGs); **`cli.md`** (`annotation-panel`, `annotation-panel-simulate` incl. `--gate-p`, `annotation-reference-prep`, `annotate` incl. standalone, `annotation-report`, `annotation-store`); **`python-api.md`** (`merxen.annotation`, `merxen.clustering`).
- **`docs/running-the-pipeline.md`:** pre-building references (`--annotation_prepare_only`, `--annotation_panel_genes_path`); store locations and manual prune; disk needs (human 2–3 GB, mouse 1.6–2 GB, 5K bundles and their transient space) [M/L]; apptainer binds for `/media` and `/srv/storage`.
- **`docs/development.md`:** slow-test variables (also `.env.example`); the vocab generator; the legacy script pin and how to update it; **the rca hook points, the integration-branch workflow and the GASTON rebase guide (§2.4)**. **`CLAUDE.md`, `Agents.md`:** the convention note (M0-docs). **`docs/acceptance/annotation-v1-preregistration.md`** (M3; MO10 variant at M6b).
- **`README.md`:** stage list; required references; licences — MerXen is MIT but depends on GPL-3.0 `leidenalg` and GPL `python-igraph`; `cell_type_mapper` is under the Allen Institute Software License (clause 3 non-commercial; matters for the redistributed apptainer image); ABC (incl. the MERFISH-638850 series used in M6b) and SEA-AD data are CC BY-NC 4.0; no other public dataset is used (OD-E1).
