| R37 | (rev2; rev3) No real data for a new panel before in-house datasets: none is downloaded by decision (OD-E1), and no public human-brain 5K dataset was found in the rev2 web search (2026-09-26) [L] | M / M | Simulation-only gate P from WHB / WMB with stricter, permanent margins; new families `provisional` until gate P; first in-house datasets get downgrade-only QC (R42–R44) | M3b, M13 |
| R38 | (rev2) Four small midbrain / pons subclasses (`157 RN Spp1 Glut`, `279 PSV Pax2 Gly-Gaba`, `280 NLL-po Pax7 Gaba`, `297 CU-ECU Pax2 Gly-Gaba`) lack 10Xv3 cells (≤ 0.2% of a posterior section) | L / L | **Accepted as a documented limitation** (user decision 2026-09-26; not filled in): listed in `docs/stages/annotation.md`, recorded in `bundle.json` and the resolvability table, and flagged in the mouse report for sections at AP ≥ 7.5 | M2 (bundle), M6 (report), M7 (docs) |
| R39 | (rev2) Declared vs observed panel mismatch (zero-count probes, crops, `min_cells` filtering) gives spurious rebuilds or different hashes in the shadow and pipeline paths | M / M | Declared-panel family hash; lookup restricted at MAP; subset bundles from kept reference markers | M3b |
| R40 | (rev2) Resolvability test cells too shallow for deep panels (non-neuronal WMB test cells almost never reach 1,000 panel counts) | H / M | Thin only cells with native counts ≥ D; D_max inheritance with `resolvability_extrapolated`; more non-neuronal mouse test cells | M3b |
| R41 | (rev2) Public validation data unusable or costly to re-segment | **withdrawn** 2026-09-26 | No public validation data are downloaded (OD-E1 / OD-E9); the simulation-only risks are R42–R44 | – |
| R42 | (rev3) **Simulation-only validation is optimistic:** native low-count cells carry 30–45% more genes than thinned cells and precision is optimistic by up to 5–10 points below ~60 counts on Xenium (E2 v5); a new panel's real precision is never measured | H / M | Stricter gate-P criteria (point precision ≥ target + 0.05, + 0.10 below 60 counts, with a Wilson bound ≥ target on ≥ 200 confident calls; coverage ≥ 0.30; frozen-threshold replicates; stress recipes; a dry run on the seeded panels); margins and max-rule floors kept after promotion; SEA second vote below 60 counts (human), mouse subclass floor 60; the gene-complexity check and downgrade-only QC on real datasets (§8.8) | M3b, M13 |
| R43 | (rev3) **Platform effects are not simulated:** measured per-platform gene factors, segmentation-driven spill-over and probe behaviour (e.g. human Xenium reads astrocytes high, 33.1% vs 23.4%, D-A7; the spill-over flag fires on 8.6% of VZG2 cells, E3) are absent from the random LogNormal / foreign-spill recipe | H / M | NP6 stress recipes incl. per-gene factors drawn from the measured human cross-platform offsets (capped ±2 log2) [L]; G1, marker referee, paired-platform concordance and flag rates on real data (downgrade-only); provisional G4 bands on Xenium; measured platform factors in v1.1 (M10) once a platform's data exist | M13, M10 |
| R44 | (rev3) **Disease / age-state mismatch:** the simulation draws neurotypical WHB donors and adult WMB mice, while in-house tissue includes Braak VI donors and aged or lesioned mouse tissue (ag7); state-driven expression shifts are not simulated (E2 caveat 1: AD-state mismatch persists at full depth and escapes self-referential checks) | M / M | SEA-AD second vote and disease-state reference (human); state genes excluded from negatives; `flag_ood`, diffuse and contamination flags; population-level disease reporting; margins kept; real-data QC can downgrade | M4, M7, M13 |
| R45 | (rev3 check) **Gate-P sample sizes:** rare classes cannot reach 200 confident calls (frontal WHB, all three donors on set a: Vascular 37 and Fibroblast 25 cells, Microglia 1,198 with 483 at ≥ 60 counts and 28 at ≥ 120 [H]; mouse Immune 0 of 80 self-map test cells at ≥ 1,000 counts), so criteria that ignore n would validate nothing, and noisy criteria would fail good panels | H / M | Pooled held-out calls, the mouse top-up, deep bins pooled and inherited (marked extrapolated), a CI-aware replicate spread, per-(level, class) validation with C_P fixed before mapping; unevaluable classes stay provisional per class and are listed; the dry run on the seeded panels shows the criteria are passable before any new family is scored | M13 |

## 17. Deliberately deferred to v2 (or later)

- **resolVI / scANVI** (GPU; scvi-tools not installed): label propagation to low-count cells; alternative representation.
- **SPLIT purification (R)** and contamination-cleaned counts for DE and cross-platform gene comparison.
- **Mouse simulation calibration** (bp + avg_correlation + margin; majority-component truth; neighbour-donor spill as a recorded parameter; ≥ 30–50k cells) and mouse supertype *leaves*; **fine reference levels as leaves** on panels that resolve them (OD-E4, decided 2026-09-26: v1 reports them only, opt-in).
- **Self-built WMB mapping means** (OD-C2), after re-validation.
- **CCF registration stage for mouse** (E7 §5 prototype): regional statistics and flags only, never relabelling.
- **Sagittal, OB- and CB-dominated mouse sections** (a sagittal region model; check whether Zhuang ABCA-3/4 cover sagittal planes).
- **CHOIR / sc-SHC benchmark** in an R container on 2–3 sections subsampled to 30–50k cells.
- **Split rule v2:** cross-platform reciprocal replication gate with a per-cell contamination score; refitting at full depth (half-depth clustering costs 0.04–0.14 ARI; E6); a mouse effect-size guard (binary-DEG count).
- **AD glial states per cell:** continuous state scores; SEA-AD glial supertypes as a within-branch level calibrated through the crosswalk; Insitutype-style semi-supervised states.
- **Platform-effect models for simulation** (gpsFISH-style) beyond M10's measured factors; **per-platform rescaling in production** if X1 is negative in v1 but promising.
- **Other human regions** (OD-C7; ROI sets, plausibility columns, held-out markers and QC per region, §8.9); **Zeng ageing mouse reference** for ag7 glial states.
- **Extra per-cell QC inputs:** nucleus-restricted counts, ovrlpy VSI, ProSeg per-cell fields (weak in E3: AUROC .42–.79), IF channels (AT8, CTSD, p62).
- **Soft spatial priors for mouse** (sensitivity only); **contract and viewer changes** (low-count objects in tables, OD-D9; viewer `proseg_hybrid` support, external repo); **annotation as an addressable stage** (OD-D3).
- **Real-data validation of new panels on public datasets** (10x 5K mouse hemisphere, Vizgen Receptor Map, Zenodo DLPFC Xenium, 10x Human Brain Preview, HMBA-MERSCOPE-H22.30.001) and the `--from-vendor-cells` input: not in v1 by your decision (OD-E1 / OD-E9, 2026-09-26); revisit only if that decision changes.

No longer deferred (rev2): Xenium 5K and custom panels (v1, §8; validated by simulation, rev3); the real-platform MERFISH-638850 series (M6b). HMBA-MERSCOPE-H22.30.001, optional in rev2's M13, is not used in rev3 (OD-E1).

## Appendix A. Review responses

**A** = accepted (where it lands); **P** = partly accepted or deferred (why); **R** = rejected. Full rev1 wording: `$A/plan/final_plan.txt`, Appendix A. Section numbers refer to this revision.

**Scientific (S).** S1 blocker (confident-only composition + Xenium Oligo floor n = 5 fail H1): **A** — floor 10 via n ≥ 50 inheritance (D-C2), soft composition with CIs (D-C5), H1 on it. S2 (flags over-fire; neurotypical negatives; AD cells excluded): **A** — dual-reference negatives minus state genes, dataset-empirical null, realised-rate bound, reference-expectation diffuse flag, report-only, DE covariate (§4.3, §5.6). S3 (leaky, circular calibration; HO off; H6 circular): **A, staged** — v1 thresholds = HO maxima (.73 / .69); the HO simulator runs in v1 as resolvability (§8.3); calibrated probabilities with measured spill and factors in v1.1 (the isotonic local-threshold step moved into v1 after the rev2 checks); H6 → HO-simulation precision + H18 + held-out genes (H4). S4 (mouse configuration unvalidated): **A** — Allen means + panel lookup exactly as validated, 10Xv3 precompute (D-M1). S5 (mouse confidence; Immune statement): **P** — grid to 2000, Immune corrected, MO4 / MO5, `avg_correlation` floor from M6; the (bp, corr, margin) simulator is v2 (no validated mouse simulator; E3 verdict 4). S6 (circular microglia / Astro-Epen acceptance): **A** — MERFISH-anchored MO4, held-out-gene MO5, spill-over framing, reseg lever (§7.4). S7 (split evidence overstated; H11): **A** — opt-in v1.1, non-trivial passes separate, held-out validation, H11 out of the flip (D-H3, §6.4). S8 (AD glia suppressed; WHB-vs-SEA weak): **P** — §5.9 statement, population disease reporting, SEA-AD glial report column (OD-B3), M3 CIs; SEA-AD supertypes are not leaves (no in-silico truth; ECE .14–.18); OD-B2 now decided. S9 (Xenium gene efficiency): **A** — X1 in M3, measured factors in v1.1, set c (D-A7). S10 (reproduction-style acceptance): **A** — pre-registration, tighten-only, held-out sets, non-circular H4 / H12 / MO4, gate fixed (§14). S11 (P1212 reclassified): **A** — E8 in M3, OD-B6. S12–S20 (minor): **A** — SEA subclass gated and `calibrated=false`; COP → lineage (D-A6); LL re-measured with (vii) (D-A5); tags and RESOLUTE wording corrected (§6.5); tier = agreement (§4.1); n ≥ 50 inheritance, CI floors in v1.1; projection validated, null check sanity-only (§6.4); MO1 fixed denominator; `flag_astro_lowcount`, pericyte-free OPC (D-M6). S-missing (13 items): all covered (§5.5, §5.8, §5.7, §8.3, §5.6, X1, §7.1, §3.7, MO4 / MO10, M3, M11, §14, §5.9).

**Engineering (E).** E-B1 (lock install cannot resolve): **A** — thin `-e` + lock-hash header, uv Dockerfile, OD-D7 (§3.8). E-M1 (`-resume` invalidation): **A** — byte-identical, pinned legacy text (D-P2). E-M2 (`uns` keys): **A** — safe tokens, JSON strings, round trip (§4.6). E-M3 (`storeDir`): **A** — ordinary PREP + `ReferenceStore` + flock (§3.2). E-M4 (MAPMYCELLS barrier): **A** — `runMapMyCells` in `rowSampleSettings` (§3.1). E-M5 (re-mapping to tune): **A** — MAP/RESOLVE split, published-output reuse. E-M6 (GPU-env imports): **A** — empty `__init__`, light modules, isolation test (M1). E-M7 (symlink cache; code version; growth): **A** — copy with checksum, `ANNOTATION_BUILDER_VERSION`, manual prune. E-X1 (metro test): **A** — per-milestone metro updates (§2.2). E-minor: **A** for all; branching is now the decided integration model (OD-D5); M0e only on annotation / MAPMYCELLS query paths. E-questions: OD-A3, OD-D5 (decided); OD-D3, OD-D4, OD-D7, OD-D8 (adopted).

**Robustness (Rb).** Rb-B1 / B2: **A** (as S2 + H16; as S1). Rb-M1 (gate C): **P** — C dropped, A pre-registered, uncalibrated predicted coverage reported from resolvability (§5.4); the composition-aware gate waits for v1.1 calibration. Rb-M2 (no mouse gate): **A** — G1–G5, invalid VZG2 as negative control; composition warning-only (OD-C4). Rb-M3 (floors): **A** — one algorithm, inheritance, unknown-panel max rule (§5.4, §8.3). Rb-M4 (grid; never-emit): **A (rev2)** — per-panel grid, `never_emit_levels` removed, one data-driven emission rule (§8.3). Rb-M5 (τ at depth): **A** — regime diagnostic, data-keyed fallback (M11). G13 variance prefilter and feature types: **A** — per-parent prefilter validated per level and class; feature types from the Xenium source table; segmentation filter bugfix (OD-D12).

**Rev3 changes (your round-2 decisions, 2026-09-26; `plan_rev3/PROGRESS_reviser_rev3.txt`, `plan_rev3/rev3_edits.py`).** (1) OD-C8 decided: the MERFISH-638850 download is approved; the selection rule picks among (a)–(d), (f) (§7.8, M6b, §10, D-M7). (2) OD-E1 / OD-E9 decided *simulation only*, not the rev2 recommendation: §8.8 rewritten; gate P and NP1–NP9 redefined as pre-registered simulation criteria with stricter margins that simulation-validated families keep in production (`validation_basis`, `validated_min_depth`; §8.2, §4.7, §3.7, D-G4, new D-G8); downgrade-only production QC on real datasets (`real_qc.py`, §8.8); M13 rescoped to 6–8 developer-days of simulation and QC work plus CPU compute (7–10 after the rev3 check), with the vendor-cell input, public downloads and re-segmentation dropped (§17); §0, §2.1, §2.2, §9, §10, §11, §13.3, the §14 rules, §15 and §18 updated; R8, R30, R33, R36 and R37 revised, R41 withdrawn, R42–R44 added (optimism, platform effects not simulated, disease / age-state mismatch); OD-D12's control-name check uses the 10x / Vizgen documentation and the first in-house dataset (§8.4, §8.6). (3) OD-E2 decided (b) and (4) OD-E4 decided (report-only, opt-in fine levels): statuses only, as the plan already used them. (5) The other rev2 open items stay open with their recommended defaults adopted unless you object. (6) Correction [H]: the default HO donor H19.30.002 has the fewest, not the most, frontal WHB cells; `"auto"` is redefined so the pilot recipe is unchanged (§3.2, §5.7). New unmeasured claims in rev3 (the NP thresholds, stress recipes, real-data QC thresholds, simulation compute, the MERFISH storage path) are marked [L].

**Rev3 check (2026-09-26; `plan_rev3/PROGRESS_verifier_rev3.txt`; fixes by `plan_rev3/rev3_fixes.py`, log `plan_rev3/PROGRESS_fixer_rev3.txt`).** V1 blocker (NP3, NP4 and NP6 could not be passed at their sample sizes: a Wilson bound ≥ 0.95 needs ≥ 73 all-correct calls, mouse Immune has 0 of 80 test cells at ≥ 1,000 counts, and a 0.03 replicate range is within sampling noise): **A**. The §14 evaluation rules now pool the held-out calls and test sets of ≥ 200 confident calls, pooling deep bins cumulatively (each cell once) and inheriting above D_P (marked extrapolated). NP3 requires point precision ≥ target⁺ with a Wilson bound ≥ target; NP6 the real-data rule under stress. NP4's replicate range must be ≤ max(0.03, 3.5 × pooled SE), not the suggested 2 × SE, which three equal replicates exceed 33% of the time by sampling alone. The constants are in §3.7 (`gate_p_*`); mouse classes are topped up to 700 test cells; and a dry run on set a, ag7 and VZG2 precedes any new family. The frontal WHB glia counts behind this were checked [H] (R45). V2 major (per-level `validated_min_depth` let one class set the depth for all, and promotion could reduce emission): **A**. Records are per (level, class) (`validated_panel_levels.csv`, §4.7) and applied in RESOLVE. A simulation-validated family emits exactly as a provisional one, so promotion changes only `ct_<L>_validated`, the banner and the warning flag (set when > 10% of confident labels fall outside the validated region). `validated_max_level` is defined on the class set C_P, fixed before mapping, and NP5's expected depth is defined (§8.2, §14). V3 (real-data QC could force `broad_only` on seeded families before gate H): **A** — warnings only on `real_data` families until their species gate; expected warnings listed in the gate PR (§8.8, §3.7, H8). V4 (M13's mouse QC needs M6b): **A** — M13 header and §2.1 row D; the C1 note updated. V5 (§5.4 warned on simulation-validated floors): **A** — `floor_source=simulation_validated`, no warning. V6 (control-filter fix came after the dataset it should protect): **A** — fixed on `main` from the vendor documentation before the first 5K or custom dataset is segmented; that dataset only confirms, with re-segmentation if a name was missed (§8.4, M3b, OD-D12, R36). V7 (Zhuang is a download): **A** — needs a separate approval (§7.8, G7). V8 (rev2 file identity; status header): **A** — Files line, §18 and status header corrected. V9 (M13 effort read as compute; components did not reach 8): **A** — developer-days; tests and integration plus the new gate-P work added: 5.5–7.5 days before contingency, 7–10 with it (§0, §2.1, M13; §10 CPU re-estimated for the mouse top-up and the dry run). V10 ("no public human-brain 5K dataset exists"): **A** — rev2's wording restored with [L] (§8.8, R37).

---

## Open decisions for the user

Every decision point is listed here, settled or not, so all the questions are in one place. **Decided** = your choice of 2026-09-26 (first round; round 2 for OD-C8, OD-E1, OD-E2, OD-E4 and OD-E9). **Adopted** = the recommendation, applied as default on 2026-09-26; you can still object. **Open** = raised in rev2 and not yet decided; per your round-2 instruction the recommended default is adopted unless you object. "Blocking" = needed before the named milestone. In total 51 decision points — A data safety and rollout (5), B human science (14, incl. OD-B14 decided 2026-09-27), C mouse (9), D engineering and process (12), E panels and platforms (11, incl. OD-E10 / OD-E11 decided 2026-09-27 in the M3b H18 follow-up); after round 2, 8 remain open, none blocking because each has an adopted default.

**Decided in round 2 (2026-09-26):** OD-C8, OD-E1 / OD-E9 (simulation only — not the rev2 recommendation), OD-E2 = (b), OD-E4. **Decided 2026-09-27 (after M3 / M3b; recorded at M4):** OD-B7 (proseg_hybrid for human, reseg for mouse), OD-B8 (no LL vote in v1.1), OD-B14 (H2 / H4 and the H7 / H18 exceptions are decided at M8), OD-E10, OD-E11. **Open, recommended default adopted unless you object:** OD-B13, OD-D11, OD-D12, OD-E3, OD-E5, OD-E6, OD-E7, OD-E8. **Adopted defaults most worth a second look:** OD-B6 (P1212 broad-only policy), OD-C4 (MERFISH composition only warns), OD-D10 (SSD1 store).

### E. Panels and platforms (new in rev2; round-2 decisions in rev3)

| ID | Question | Options | Status / recommendation | Evidence / trade-off |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| OD-E1 | Which data validate new panels before in-house data are trusted? (blocking: M13) | the §8.8 public set (rev2) / a subset / in-house data only / simulation only | **Decided 2026-09-26 (round 2): simulation only** — not the rev2 recommendation (the public set). New panels are validated only by in-silico simulation from the Allen references (gate P: pre-registered simulation criteria NP1–NP9 with stricter margins, scored per (level, class) and kept in production; §8.8, §14); no public validation dataset is downloaded (not the 10x 5K mouse hemisphere, Vizgen Receptor Map, Zenodo DLPFC, 10x Human Brain preview or HMBA-MERSCOPE); MERFISH-638850 serves only the AP-axis rule (OD-C8); real in-house datasets get label-free production QC that can only downgrade or warn | Cheap, reproducible and needs no new data, but optimistic (E2 v5: native low-count cells +30–45% genes; up to 5–10 points below ~60 counts on Xenium) and blind to platform effects and disease / age state, so the margins are stricter and permanent and a new panel's real accuracy is never measured (R42–R44) |
| OD-E2 | Promote the v1.1 simulation calibration (M10) into v1 for panels without real-data validation? (blocking: M3b) | (a) M10 wholesale / (b) conservative rule + HO simulator with E2's local isotonic thresholds and composition reweighting (raise-only thresholds, max-rule floors, provisional margins, NP acceptance) / (c) conservative rule without simulation | **Decided 2026-09-26 (round 2): (b)** — simulation-derived thresholds and floors in v1 (HO simulator, E2 local isotonic raise-only thresholds, composition reweighting, max-rule floors, provisional margins; §8.9); calibrated-probability output, measured contamination and platform factors stay v1.1 (M10) | Thresholds and floors are panel-specific, so (c) is unsafe; set-level thresholds miss targets in 37–50% of combinations, the local rule in none (E2 v3); the 44% real-vs-sim figure is for the recipe (b) uses and rises to 78% with reweighting, which (b) includes; (a)'s remaining parts need per-platform measurements that do not exist before data |
| OD-E3 | How to annotate pairs with different panels? (blocking: M3b) | auto (intersection if Jaccard ≥ 0.9, else per platform + intersection runs) / always intersection / always per platform | **Open; recommended default adopted unless you object: auto** (§8.5) | Always-intersection discards most of a 5K panel; always-per-platform breaks set a / set c for same-panel pairs |
| OD-E4 | May deep panels (5K) emit finer levels (WHB cluster, WMB supertype)? (blocking: M3b) | report-only opt-in columns when resolvability + seed stability pass / leaves when resolvable / never in v1 | **Decided 2026-09-26 (round 2): report-only opt-in columns** (`annotation_allow_fine_levels`, default false) when resolvability and seed stability pass (§8.3); hierarchy leaves stay WHB supercluster / WMB subclass; fine levels as leaves stay v2 (§17) | Keeps MENDER keys and cross-platform comparisons stable (OD-B1); WHB cluster coverage ≤ 5.7% on the 296 panel; high seed noise at fine levels |
| OD-E5 | What does a `provisional` panel emit before NP acceptance? (blocking: M3b) | resolvability-derived levels with targets +0.05 (+0.10 below 60 counts, capped 0.97; mouse subclass floor 60), gate warning flag and banner (gate level unchanged), separate cohort listing / broad-only until NP / refuse until NP | **Open; recommended default adopted unless you object: the first**, with the §8.2 refused / broad-only thresholds pre-registered; families validated by simulation keep the same margins and emission rule, so promotion never changes what is emitted (OD-E1, §8.2) | Fails safe without discarding a panel the simulation shows can resolve; E2 measured 5–10 points of optimism below ~60 counts |
| OD-E6 | Storage and caching for panels > 1,000 genes (blocking: M3b) | `/srv/storage` large store, family bundles keyed by the declared panel with reference markers kept, per-parent prefilter / everything on SSD1 / delete reference markers after query markers | **Open; recommended default adopted unless you object: the first** (§8.7); prefilter validated per level and class (≥ 0.95 per class with n ≥ 50, no parent < 5 markers) by simulation in M3b and gate P (NP9), and spot-checked on the first in-house 5K dataset (downgrade-only) | Reference-marker h5 grows linearly (5.9 / 9.4 GB at 500 / 815 genes → ~60 GB at 5K [L]); deleting them turns any gene-set difference into a full rebuild; SSD1 has 560 GB free, `/srv/storage` 4.4 TB |
| OD-E7 | (new) When does a panel inherit a validated family's trust? (blocking: M3b) | same species and platform + Jaccard ≥ 0.95 + all root markers / Jaccard + root markers only / exact hash only | **Open; recommended default adopted unless you object: the first** | Without the platform condition a Xenium panel resembling VZG2 would inherit MERSCOPE `validated`; exact-hash-only would demote set a's post-M0e hash (297 vs 296 genes) |
| OD-E8 | (new) Shared-host memory exception for 5K reference prep (blocking: first 5K PREP) | allow PREP for > 1,000-gene panels the M3b-measured peak + 30% (probably 128–192 GB), `maxForks 1`, prefilter mandatory above that / always require the prefilter and stay near 64 GB / run 5K prep on another host | **Open; recommended default adopted unless you object: the first**, measured at 8 processes in M3b before any 5K run; *M3b measurement (2026-09-27): the 5K WMB marker steps peak at 37.7 GB prefiltered and 21.3 GB unfiltered (8 processes, `--max_gb 40`), + 30% = 49 GB, within the standard 64 GB reserve; `annotation_prep_large_memory = "64 GB"`, the prefilter opt-in (it failed its subclass validation) and mandatory only above the reserve, so no memory exception is needed for 5K panels (`m3b/M3B_EXIT_REPORT.txt`)* | WMB query markers peak 20.2 / 27.1 GB at 500 / 815 genes (~22 MB per gene → ~120–170 GB at 5K, ~53–66 GB with the prefilter [L]); host 739 GB; etiquette ≤ 40 GB per job |
| OD-E9 | (new) Downloads, storage and compute for the M13 public datasets (blocking: M13) | list files, sizes and licences first, then approve per dataset; store under `/srv/storage/MerXen/public_validation/`; vendor cells first, full pipeline on one dataset per platform / download all and run the full pipeline on each / in-silico only | **Decided 2026-09-26 (round 2), with OD-E1: in-silico only** — no public validation downloads, storage or re-segmentation; M13 compute is CPU simulation (§10); the vendor-cell input is dropped (§17) | Re-segmentation would have cost ~3–6 GPU-h + 6–10 CPU-h per section (M0a rate) and the layouts were partly unverified; the simulation needs only the Allen references the pipeline already uses |
| OD-E10 | (M3b H18 follow-up) Deep depth bins with fewer than 50 confident calls although D_max(c) has ≥ 50 test cells (set a Oligo at 120 / 250, 42 calls, all correct) | keep (not emitted) / inherit from the deepest decided bin / pool deep bins as gate P does | **Decided 2026-09-27: pool deep bins** — from the deep end, each cell once at its deepest bin, until 50 confident calls; the pooled set judged by the gate-P rule (point precision and Wilson bound on the Kish n; one rule for §8.3 and gate P, §14); the verdict carried to every pooled bin, flagged `resolvability_extrapolated`; classes never reaching 50 calls even fully pooled `not_resolvable` (`insufficient_calls`) (§8.3) | M3b stage C / review fix: `m3b/resolvability/STAGE_C_REPORT.txt`, `m3b/review_fix/REVIEW_FIX_REPORT.txt`. Measured (`m3b/followup/H18_FOLLOWUP.txt`): pooling alone leaves one set a H18 exception (broad OPC at 15) and lifts the H7 proxy on P7513 MERSCOPE from .532 to .638 (≥ .62); in RESOLVE a pooled set is reweighted as one set to the dataset's cells at ≥ D_P. *M3b review 2 (resolvability version 4, `m3b/review2/REVIEW2_H18.txt`):* weights trimmed per tested set, judged bins at or above D_P keep their own verdict, zero-weight calls not counted and the held-out truths no call can name left out; the P7513 MERSCOPE H7 proxy is then .683 (≥ .62) and the MERSCOPE Oligo 120 exceptions disappear; P5011 MERSCOPE's H7 proxy is .297 (< .30) in the version-4 simulation draw (.311 on the version-3 draw). *M3b final follow-up and review 3 (2026-09-28, resolvability version 6, `m3b/final_followup/FINAL_FOLLOWUP.txt` §12):* every per-cell simulation draw keyed per cell, so a changed test set no longer redraws the other cells, with the pre-registered seed-0 gene efficiency (version 5 had also redrawn the efficiency, keyed per gene id, which neither change needed; its H7 pass on P5011 MERSCOPE, .380, came from that redraw). Under version 6 the H7 proxy misses on P5011 MERSCOPE (.298 < .30) and passes on the other seven (P1212 MERSCOPE .354 ≥ .34), and 23 reweighted (level, class) pairs fail H18 (version 4: 25; broad OPC at 15 on all eight datasets). A 3 × 3 per-cell seed × efficiency seed grid on set a (`review3/FACTORIAL_ANALYSIS.txt`) gives P5011 MERSCOPE .297–.431 (below target in 2 of 9 cells, both with the seed-0 efficiency) and 12–26 reweighted failures; neither factor is significant for H7 or the H18 counts in that grid, and only broad OPC at 15 clearly follows the efficiency draw. *Open (user):* whether H18 / H7 are judged on the pre-registered seed-0 draw, on several draws, or on the worst of them |
| OD-E11 | (M3b H18 follow-up) Human thin non-neuronal classes in the held-out test set (HO donor: Vascular 23, Fibroblast 5, COP 20 test cells; never emitted) | accept / add cells from other frontal donors (leaks into the HO reference) / add non-neuronal cells from WHB neocortex outside the frontal reference, as E2 did | **Decided 2026-09-27: other-region cells** — non-neuronal WHB nuclei of E2's 14 neocortical dissections outside the frontal ROIs, disjoint from every reference / marker cell, capped per supercluster like the rest, recorded in the bundle (§8.3 step 1) | E2 `research/insilico/01b_extract_nonneurons.py`. Measured (`m3b/followup/H18_FOLLOWUP.txt`): +2,955 test cells (Vascular 678, Fibroblast 438, COP 284 in all); set a now emits broad Vascular (10–250) and Fibroblast (15–250), but other-region COP cells called Oligodendrocyte make broad / supercluster Oligo unresolvable at 120 counts and lower the P7513 MERSCOPE H7 proxy to .576 (< .62); 694 drawn cells are of clusters the HO training reference lacks (without them: .611). *Further user decision 2026-09-27, implemented 2026-09-28 (`HO_OTHER_REGION_VERSION` 2, `m3b/final_followup/FINAL_FOLLOWUP.txt`):* the top-up draws only from clusters the HO training reference keeps (≥ 5 training cells); set a: 2,263 other-region cells, 852 candidates of 27 absent clusters left out and recorded, so Vascular / Fibroblast / COP hold 412 / 144 / 152 test cells; the remaining broad and supercluster Oligo exceptions are other-region COP cells of training clusters called Oligodendrocyte (9–26% of the called set's confident weight under resolvability version 6; 3–25% in version 5's draw) |
